Suśrutasaṃhitā (without commentaries) transcribed by Tsutomu Yamashita and Yasutaka Muroya on the basis of Y. T. Ācārya's 1931 and 1938 Bombay editions
The Suśrutasaṃhitā of Suśruta, with the Nibandhsangraha Commentary of Śrī Ḍalhaṇācārya.
Yādavaśarman Trivikramātmaja Ācārya
Pāndurang Jāvaji
Bombay
1931
Revised second edition
The sūtrasthāna (Su.1) and śārīrasthāna (Su.3) of this SARIT edition are mainly based on this edition.
This SARIT edition omits the commentarial material from this edition.
Suśrutasamhitā of Suśruta with the Nibandhasaṅgraha Commentary of Śrī Ḍalhanāchārya and the Nyāyacandrikā Pañjikā of Śrī Gayadāsāchārya on Nidānasthāna, edited from the begining to the 9th ādhyāya of Cikitsāsthāna by Vaidya Jādavji Trikamji Āchārya and the rest by Nārāyaṇ Rām Āchārya
Nirṇaya Sāgar Press
Bombay
1938
Reprint edition Varanasi/Delhi: Chaukhambha Orientalia, 1992.
The nidānasthāna (Su.2), cikitsāsthāna (Su.4) , kalpasthāna (Su.5) and uttaratantra (Su.6) are based on this edition
This SARIT edition omits the commentarial material from this edition.
The published edition from which this e-text was transcribed is printed in the Devanāgarī script. The electronic text below is in a lossless transliteration using the Latin alphabet. The transliteration scheme used is the IAST (
The International Alphabet of Sanskrit Transliteration
). IAST differs in small ways from ISO 15919, but is preferred by most working Sanskrit scholars. Conversion of this file to ISO 15919 can be achieved by performing the following replacements throughout the file:
ṛ -> r̥ and ṃ -> ṁ
Text division is as Devanāgarī ("ityevam" not "ity evam".)
Only some of the footnotes from the edition have been typed in this file.
Prose passages are enclosed in ab-elements. Verses were enclosed in lg- and l-elements, but this is not consistent. So it is possible to find verses that are either not marked up as such or enclosed in an ab-element.
Initial vowel elision for avagraha is reversed and marked with a + sign: e.g., "prathamo+adhyāyaḥ"
Ver.1. August 2009 completed and released by Yamashita and Muroya
Added TEI encoding.
Added Su.1.12 and Su.1.14 that had somehow gone missing (probably my fault).
recoded the footnotes that were marked with & using XML "note" elements
Recoded the numerous ampersands as XML "ref" entities. Some of these refer to footnotes that have been typed into this file, but many are just floating, when the corresponding footnote has not been typed.
Wrapped prose elements in ab-elements and l-elements in lg's.
Wrapped passage numbers in label-elements.
Added subtype-attribute to head-elements.
Wrapped trailers in trailer-elements.
Changed some ab-elements to lg-elements for verses that were wrongly tagged as prose.

Su.1.1.4/ bhagavan śārīramānasāgantuvyādhibhirvividhavedanābhighātopadrutān sanāthānapyanāthavadviceṣṭamānān vikrośataśca mānavānabhisamīkṣya manasi naḥ pīḍā bhavati teṣāṃ sukhaiṣiṇāṃ rogopaśamārthamātmanaśca prāṇayātrārthaṃ pragāhitahetorāyurvedaṃ śrotumicchāma ihopadiśyamānaṃ atrā+āyattamaihikamāmuṣmikaṃ ca śreyaḥ tadbhagavantamupapannāḥ smaḥ śiṣyatveneti //

SSu.3.8.26/ avagāḍhe jalaukāḥ syāt pracchānaṁ piṇḍite hitam// sirā+aṅgavyāpake rakte śṛṅgālābū tvaci sthite//

<B 10>

daśamo 'dhyāyaḥ |

<B 76>

SL15/ daśamo 'dhyāyaḥ /
SSu.3.10.1/ athāto garbhiṇīvyākaraṇaṃ śārīraṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ / /
SSu.3.10.2/ yathovāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ / /
SSu.3.10.4/ viśeṣatas tu garbhiṇī prathamadvitīyatṛtīyamāseṣu madhuraśītadravaprāyamāhāram upaseveta, viśeṣatas tu tṛtīye ṣaṣṭikaudanaṃ payasā bhojayeccaturthe dadhnā pañcame payasā ṣaṣṭhe sarpiṣā cetyeke, caturthe payonavanītasaṃsṛṣṭamāhārayejjāṅgalamāṃsasahitaṃ hṛdyam annaṃ bhojayet , pañcame kṣīrasarpiḥsaṃsṛṣṭaṃ , ṣaṣṭhe śvaśvadaṃṣṭrāsiddhasya sarpiṣo mātrāṃ pāyayed yavāgūṃ vā, saptame sarpiḥ pṛthakparṇyādisiddham, evam āpyāyate garbhaḥ; aṣṭame badarodakena balātibalāśatapuṣpāpalalapayodadhimastutailalavaṇamadanaphalamadhughṛtamiśreṇāsthāpayet purāṇapurīṣaśuddhyartham anulomanārthaṃ ca vāyoḥ, tataḥ payomadhurakaṣāyasiddhena tailenānuvāsayet, anulome hi vāyau sukhaṃ prasūyate nirupadravā ca bhavati, ata ūrdhvaṃ snigdhābhir yavāgbhir jāṅgalarasaiścopakramedāprasavakālāt; evam upakrāntā snigdhā balavatī sukhamanupadravā prasūyate / /
SSu.3.10.5/ navame māsi sūtikāgāramenāṃ praveśayet praśastatithyādau, tatrāriṣṭaṃ brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyaśūdrāṇāṃ śvetaraktapītakṛṣṇeṣu bhūmipradeśeṣu bilvanyagrodhatindukabhallātaka-nirmitaṃ sarvāgāraṃ yathāsaṅkhyaṃ tanmayaparyaṅkam upaliptabhittiṃ suvibhaktaparicchadaṃ prāgdvāraṃ kakṣiṇadvāraṃ vā 'ṣṭahastāyataṃ caturhastavistṛtaṃ rakṣāmaṅgalasaṃpannaṃ vidheyam / /
SSu.3.10.6/ jāte hi śithile kukṣau mukte hṛdayabandhane / / saśūle jaghane vārī jñeyā sā tu prajāyinī / /
SSu.3.10.7/ tatropasthitaprasavāyāḥ kaṭīpṛṣṭhaṃ prati samantād vedanā bhavaty abhīkṣṇaṃ purīṣapravṛttir mūtraṃ prasicyate yonimukhāc chleṣmā ca / /
SSu.3.10.8/ prajanayiṣyamāṇāṃ kṛtamaṅgalasvastivācanāṃ kumāraparivṛtāṃ punnāmaphalahastāṃ svabhyaktāmuṣṇodakapariṣiktāmathaināṃ sambhṛtāṃ yavāgūmākaṇṭhāt pāyayet, tataḥ kṛtopadhāne mṛduni vistīrṇe śayane sthitāmābhugnasakthīmuttānāmaśaṅkanīyāścatasraḥ striyaḥ pariṇatavayasaḥ prajananakuśalāḥ kartitanakhāḥ paricareyuriti / /
SSu.3.10.9/ athāsyā viśikhāntaramanulomam-asunukhamabhyajyānubrūyāc caināmikā ---subhage pravāhasveti, nacāprāptāvī pravāhasva, tato vimukte garbhanāḍīprabandhe saśūleṣu śroṇivaṅkṣaṇabastiśiraḥsu ca pravāhethāḥ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ (pūrvaṃ ), tato garbhanirgame pragāḍhaṃ, tato garbhe yonimukhaṃ prapanne gāḍhataramāviśalyabhāvāt; akālapravāhaṇādbadhiraṃ mūkaṃ kubjaṃ vyastahanumūrdhvābhighātinaṃ kāsaśvāsaśoṣopadrutaṃ vikaṭaṃ vā janayati / /
SSu.3.10.10/ tatra pratilomam anulomayet / /
SSu.3.10.11/ garbhasaṅge tu yoniṃ dhūpayet kṛṣṇasarpanirmokeṇa piṇḍītakena vā, badhnīyād dhiraṇyapuṣpīmūlaṃ hastapādayoḥ, dhārayet suvarcalāṃ viśalyāṃ vā / /
SSu.3.10.12/ atha jātasyolbamapanīya , mukhaṃ ca saindhavasarpiṣā viśodhya, ghṛtāktaṃ mūrdhni picuṃ dadyāt; tato nābhivāḍīmaṣṭāṅgulam āyamya sūtreṇa baddhvā chedayet, tat sūtraikadeśaṃ ca kumārasya grīvāyāṃ samyag badhnīyāt / /
SSu.3.10.13/ atha kumāraṃ śītābhir adbhir āśvāsya jātakarmaṇi kṛte madhusarpiranantacūrṇam aṅgulyā 'nāmikayā lehayet; tato balātailenābhyajya, kṣīravṛkṣakaṣāyeṇa sarvagandhodakena vā rūpyahemaprataptena vā vāriṇā snāpayed enaṃ kapitthapatrakaṣāyeṇa vā koṣṇena yathākālaṃ yathādoṣaṃ yathāvibhavaṃ ca / /
SSu.3.10.14/ dhamanīnāṃ hṛdisthānāṃ vivṛtatvād anantaram / / catūrātrāt trirātrād vā strīṇāṃ stanyaṃ pravartate / /
SSu.3.10.15/ tasmāt prathame 'hni madhusarpir-anantamiśraṃ mantrapūtaṃ trikālaṃ pāyayet, dvitīye lakṣmaṇāsiddhaṃ sarpiḥ, tṛtīye ca; tataḥ prāṅgivāritastanyaṃ madhusarpiḥ svapāṇitalasaṃmitaṃ dvikālaṃ pāyayet / /
SSu.3.10.16/ atha sūtikāṃ balātailābhyaktāṃ vātaharauṣadhaniṣkvāthenopacaret / saśeṣadoṣāṃ tu tad ahaḥ pippalīpippalīmūlahastipippalīcitrakaśṛṅgaveracūrṇaṃ guḍodakenoṣṇena pāyayet, evaṃ dvirātraṃ trirātraṃ vā kuryād āduṣṭaśoṇitāt / viśuddhe tato vidārigandhādisiddhāṃ snehayavāgūṃ kṣīrayavāgūṃ vā pāyayet trirātram / tato yavakolakulatthasiddhena śālyodanaṃ bhojayed balam agnibalaṃ cāvekṣya / anena vidhinā 'dhyardhamāsam upasaṃskṛtā vimuktāhārācārā vigatasūtikābhidhānā syāt, punar ārtavadarśanād ity eke / /
SSu.3.10.17/ dhavvabhūmijātāṃ tu sūtikāṃ ghṛtatailayor anyatarasya mātrāṃ pāyayet pippalyādikaṣāyānupānāṃ, snehan ity ā ca syāt trirātraṃ pañcarātraṃ vā (balavatī ;) abalāṃ yavāgūṃ pāyayet trirātraṃ pañcarātraṃ vā / ata ūrdhvaṃ snigdhenānnasaṃsargeṇopacaret / /
SSu.3.10.18/ prāyaśaś caināṃ prabhūtenoṣṇodakena pariṣiñcet, krodhāyāsamaithunādīn pariharet / /
SSu.3.10.19/ bhavataś cātra / mithyācārāt sūtikāyā yo vyādhirupajāyate / / sa kṛcchrasādhyo 'sādhyo vā bhaved atyapatarpaṇāt / /
SSu.3.10.20/ tasmāt tāṃ deśakālau ca vyādhisātmyena karmaṇā / / parīkṣyopacaren nityam evaṃ nātyayam āpnuyāt / /
SSu.3.10.21/ athāparā 'patanty ānāhādhmānau kurute, tasmāt kaṇṭhamasyāḥ keśaveṣṭitayā 'ṅgulyā pramṛjet, kaṭukālābukṛtavedhanasarṣapasarpanirmokair vā kaṭutailavimiśrair yonimukhaṃ dhūpayet, lāṅgalīmūlakalkena vā 'syāḥ pāṇipādatalam ālimpet, mūrdhni vā 'syā mahāvṛkṣakṣīram anusecayet, kuṣṭhalāṅgalīmūlakalkaṃ vā madyamūtrayor anyatareṇa pāyayet, śālamūlakalkaṃ vā pippalyādiṃ vā madyena, siddhārthakakuṣṭhalāṅgalīmahāvṛkṣakṣīramiśreṇa surāmaṇḍena vā 'sthāpayet, etair eva siddhena siddhārthakatailenottarabastiṃ dadyāt, snigdhena vā kṛttanakhena hastenāpaharet / /
SSu.3.10.22/ prajātāyāś ca nāryā rukṣaśarīrāyāstīkṣṇair-aviśodhitaṃ raktaṃ vāyunā taddeśagatenātisaṃruddhaṃ nābheradhaḥ pārśvayor bastau bastiśirasi vā granthiṃ karoti; tataś ca nābhibastyudaraśūlāni bhavanti, sūcībhir iva nistudyate bhidyate dīryata iva ca pakvāśayaḥ, samantādādhmānamudare mūtrasaṅgaś ca bhavatīti makkallalakṣaṇam / tatra (sarpiṣā sukhoṣṇena lavaṇacūrṇena vā) vīratarvādisiddhaṃ jalamuṣakādipratīvāpaṃ pāyayet, yavakṣāracūrṇaṃ vā pippalyādikvāthena, pippalyādicūrṇaṃ vā surāmaṇḍena, varuṇādikvāthaṃ vā pañcakolailāpratīvāpaṃ, pṛthakparṇyādikvāthaṃ vā bhadradārumaricasaṃsṛṣṭaṃ, purāṇaguḍaṃ vā trikaṭukacaturjātakakustumburumuśraṃ khādet, acchaṃ vā pibed ariṣṭam iti / /
SSu.3.10.23/ atha bālaṃ kṣaumaparivṛtaṃ kṣaumavastrāstṛtāyāṃ śayyāyāṃ śāyayet, pīlubadarīnimbaparūṣakaśākhābhiś cainaṃ parivījayet, mūrdhni cāsyāharahastailapicum avacārayet, dhūpayec cainaṃ rakṣoghnair dhūpaiḥ, rakṣoghnāni cāsya pāṇipādaśirogrīvāsv avasṛjet, tilātasīsarṣapakaṇāṃś cātra prakiret, adhiṣṭhāne cāgniṃ prajvālayet, vraṇitopāsanīyaṃ cāvekṣeta / /
SSu.3.10.24/ tato daśame 'hani mātāpitarau kṛtamaṅgalakautukau svastivācanaṃ kṛtvā nāma kuryāt āṃ yad abhipretaṃ nakṣatranāma vā / /
SSu.3.10.25/ tato yathāvarṇaṃ dhātrīmupeyānmadhyamapramāṇāṃ madhyamavayaskāmarogāṃ śīlavatīmacapalāmalolupāmakṛśāmasthūlāṃ prasannakṣīrāmalambauṣṭhīmalambordhvastanīmavyaṅgāmavyasaninīṃ jīvadvatsāṃ dogdhrīṃ vatsalāmakṣudrakarmiṇīṃ kule jātāmato bhūyiṣṭhaiś ca guṇairanvitāṃ śyāmāmārogyabalavṛddhaye bālasya / tatrordhvastanī karālaṃ kuryāt , lambastanī nāsikāmukhaṃ chādayitvā maraṇam āpādayet / tataḥ praśastāyāṃ tithau śiraḥsnātam ahatavāsasam udaṅmukhaṃ śiśum upaveśya dhātrīṃ prāṅmukhīm upaveśya dakṣiṇaṃ stanaṃ dhautam īṣatparisrutam abhimantrya mantreṇānena pāyayet / /
SSu.3.10.26/ `catvāraḥ sāgarās tubhyaṃ stanayoḥ kṣīravāhiṇaḥ / bhavantu subhage nityaṃ bālasya balavṛddhaye / /
SSu.3.10.27/ payo 'mṛtarasaṃpītvā kumāras te śubhānane / dīrgham āyur avāpnotu devāḥ prāśyāmṛtaṃ yathā / / '
SSu.3.10.28/ ato 'nyathā nānāstanyopayogasyāsātmyād vyādhijanma bhavati / /
SSu.3.10.29/ aparisrute 'py atistabdhastanyapūrṇastanapānādutsuhitasrotasaḥ śiśoḥ kāsaśvāsavamī prādurbhāvaḥ / tasmād evaṃ vidhānāṃ stanyaṃ na pāyayet / /
SSu.3.10.30/ krodhaśokāvātsalyādibhiś ca striyāḥ stanyanāśo bhavati / athāsyāḥ kṣīrajananārthaṃ saumanasyam utpādya yavagodhūmaśāliṣaṣṭikamāṃsarasasurāsauvīrakapiṇyākalaśunamatsyakaśerukaśṛṅgāṭakabisavidārikandamadhukaśatāvarīnalikālābūkālaśākaprabhṛtīni vidadhyāt / /
SSu.3.10.31/ athāsyāḥ stanyam apsu parīkṣeta, tac cecchītalam amalaṃ tanu śaṅkhāvabhāsam apsu nyastam ekībhāvaṃ gacchaty aphenilam atantum annotplavate 'vasīdati vā tac chuddham iti vidyāt, tena kumārasyārogyaṃ śarīropacayo balavṛddhiś ca bhavati / na ca kṣudhitaśokārtaśrāntapraduṣṭadhātugarbhiṇījvaritātikṣīṇātisthūlavidagdhabhaktaviruddhāhāratarpitāyāḥ stanyaṃ pāyayet; nājīrṇauṣadhaṃ ca bālaṃ, doṣauṣadhamalānāṃ tīvravegotpattibhayāt / /
SSu.3.10.32/ bhavanti cātra / dhātryās tu gurubhir bhojyair viṣamair doṣalais tathā / / doṣā dehe prakupyanti tataḥ stanyaṃ praduṣyati / /
SSu.3.10.33/ mithyāhāravihāriṇya duṣṭā vātādayaḥ striyāḥ / / dūṣayanti payas tena śārīrā vyādhayaḥ śiśoḥ / / bhavanti kuśalas tāṃś ca bhiṣak samyag vibhāvayet / /
SSu.3.10.34/ aṅgapratyaṅgadeśe tu rujā yatrāsya jāyate / / muhurmuhuḥ spṛśati taṃ spṛśyamāne ca roditi / /
SSu.3.10.35/ nimīlitākṣo mūrdhasthe śiro roge na dhārayet / / bastis the mūtrasaṅgārto rujā tṛṣyati mūrcchati / /
SSu.3.10.36/ viṇmūtrasaṅgavaivarṇyacchardyādhmānāntrakūjanaiḥ / / koṣṭhe doṣān vijānīyāt sarvatrasthāṃś ca rodanaiḥ / /
SSu.3.10.37/ teṣu ca yathābhihitaṃ mṛdv acchedanīyam auṣadhaṃ mātrayā kṣīrapasya kṣīrasarpiṣā dhātryāś ca vidadhyāt, kṣīrānnād asyātmani dhātryāś ca, annādasya kaṣāyādīn ātmany eva na dhātryāḥ / /
SSu.3.10.38/ tatra māsādūrdhvaṃ kṣīrapāyāṅguliparvadvayagraha(ṇa)saṃmitāmauṣadhamātrāṃ vidadhyāt, kolāsthisaṃmitāṃ kalkamātrāṃ kṣīrānnādāya, kolasaṃmitāmannādāyeti / /
SSu.3.10.39/ yeṣāṃ gadānāṃ ye yogāḥ pravakṣyante 'gadaṅkarāḥ / / teṣu tat kalkasaṃliptau pāyayeta śiśuṃ stanau / /
SSu.3.10.40/ ekaṃ dve trīṇi cāhāni vātapittakaphajvare / / stanyapāyāhitaṃ sarpiritarābhyāṃ yathārthataḥ / /
SSu.3.10.41/ na ca tṛṣṇābhayād atra pāyayeta śiśuṃ stanau / / virekabastivamanānyṛte kuryāc ca nātyayāt / /
SSu.3.10.42/ mastuluṅgakṣayād yasya vāyus tālv asthi nāmayet / / tasya tṛḍdainyayuktasya sarpirmadhurakaiḥ śṛtam / /
SSu.3.10.43/ pānābhyañjanayor yojyaṃ śītāmbūdvejanaṃ tathā / / vātenādhmāpitāṃ nābhiṃ sarujāṃ tuṇḍisaṃjñitām / /
SSu.3.10.44/ mārutaghnaiḥ praśamayet snehasvedopanāhanaiḥ / / gudapāke tu bālānāṃ pittaghnīṃ kārayet kriyām / / rasāñjanaṃ viśeṣeṇa pānālepanayor hitam / /
SSu.3.10.45/ kṣīrāhārāya sarpiḥ pāyayet siddhārthakavacāmāṃsīpayasyāpāmārgaśatāvarīsārivābrāhmīpippapīharidrākuṣṭhasaindhavasiddhaṃ, kṣīrānnādāya madhukavacāpippalīcitrakatriphalāsiddham, annādāya dvipañcamūlīkṣīratagarabhadradārumaricamadhukaviḍaṅga-drākṣādvibrāhmīsiddaṃ ; tenārogyabalamedhāyūṃṣi śiśorbhavanti / /
SSu.3.10.46/ bālaṃ punar gātrasukhaṃ gṛhṇīyāt, na cainaṃ tarjayet, sahasā na pratibodhayed vitrāsabhayāt, sahasā nāpahared utkṣiped vā vātādivighātabhayāt, nopaveśayet kaubjyabhayāt, nityaṃ cainam anuvarteta priyśatair ajighāṃsuḥ; evam anabhihatamanās tv abhivardhate nityam udagrasat tv asaṃpanno nīrogaḥ suprasannamanāś ca bhavati / vātātapavidyutprabhāpādapalatāśūnyāgāranimnasthānagra(gṛ?)hacchāyādibhyo durgrahopasargataś ca bālaṃ rakṣet / /
SSu.3.10.47/ nāśucau visṛjed bālaṃ nākāśe viṣame na ca / / noṣmamārutavarṣeṣu rajodhūmodakeṣu ca / /
SSu.3.10.48/ kṣīrasātmyatayā kṣīramājaṃ gavyam athāpi vā / / dadyād āstanyaparyāpter bālānāṃ vīkṣya mātrayā / /
SSu.3.10.49/ ṣaṇmāsaṃ cainamannaṃ prāśayel laghu hitaṃ ca / /
SSu.3.10.50/ nityam avarodharataś ca syāt kṛtarakṣa upasargabhayāt; prayatnataś ca grahopasargebhyo rakṣyā bālā bhavanti / /
SSu.3.10.51/ atha kumāra udvijate trasyati roditi naṣṭasaṃjño bhavati nakhadaśanairghātrīmātmānaṃ ca pariṇudati dantān khādati kūjati jṛmbhate bhruvau vikṣipaty ūrdhvaṃ nirīkṣate phenam udvamati sandaṣṭauṣṭhaḥ kūro bhinnāmavarcā dīnārtasvaro niśi jāgarti durbalo mlānāṅgo matsyaccahucchrundarimatkuṇagandho yathā purā dhātryāḥ stanyam abhilaṣati tathā nābhilaṣatīti sāmānyena grahopasṛṣṭalakṣaṇam uktaṃ, vistareṇottare vakṣyāmaḥ / /
SSu.3.10.52/ śaktimantaṃ cainaṃ jñātvā yathāvarṇaṃ vidyāṃ grāhayet / /
SSu.3.10.53/ athāsmai pañcaviṃśativarṣāya dvādaśavarṣāṃ patnīm āvahet pitryadharmārthakāmaprajāḥ prāpsyatīti / /
SSu.3.10.54/ ūnaṣoḍaśavarṣāyām aprāptaḥ pañcaviṃśatim / / yady ādhatte umān garbhaṃ kukṣisthaḥ sa vipadyate / /
SSu.3.10.55/ jāto vā na ciraṃ jīvej jīved vā durbalendriyaḥ / / tasmād atyantabālāyāṃ garbhādhānaṃ na kārayet / /
SSu.3.10.56/ ativṛddhāyāṃ dīrgharogiṇyām anyena vā vikāreṇopasṛṣṭāyāṃ garbhādhānaṃ naiva kurvīta / puruṣasyāpy evaṃvidhasya ta eva doṣāḥ saṃbhavanti / /
SSu.3.10.57/ tatra pūrvoktaiḥ patiṣyati garbhe garbhāśayakaṭīvaṅkṣaṇabastiśūlāni raktadarśanaṃ ca, tatra śītaiḥ pariṣekāvagāhapradehādibhir upacarejjīvanīyaśṛtakṣīrapānaiś ca; garbhasphuraṇe muhurmuhus tat sandhāraṇārthaṃ kṣīram utpalādisiddhaṃ pāyayet; prasraṃsamāne sadāhapārśvapṛṣṭhaśūlāsṛgdarānāhamūtrasaṅgāḥ, sthānāt sthānaṃ copakrāmati garbhe koṣṭhe saṃrambhaḥ, tatra snigdhaśītāḥ kriyāḥ; vedanāyāṃ mahāsahākṣudrasahāmadhukaśvadaṃṣṭrākaṇṭakārikāsiddhaṃ payaḥ śarkarākṣaudramiśraṃ pāyayet; mūtrasaṅge darbhādisiddhaṃ; ānāhe hiṅgusauvarcalalaśunavacāsiddhaṃ; atyarthaṃ sravati rakte koṣṭhāgārikāgāramṛtpiṇḍasamaṅgādhātakīkusumanavamālikāgairikasarjarasarasāñjanacūrṇaṃ madhunā 'valihyāt, yathālābhaṃ nyagrodhāditvakpravālakalkaṃ vā payasā pāyayet, utpalādikalkaṃ vā kaśeruśṛṅgāṭakaśālūkakalkaṃ vā śṛtena payasā, udumbaraphalaudakakandakvāthena vā śarkarābhadhumadhureṇa śālipiṣṭaṃ, nyagrodhādisvarasaparipītaṃ vā vastrāvayavaṃ yonyāṃ dhārayet; athādṛṣṭaśoṇitavedanāyāṃ madhukadevadārumañjiṣṭhāpayasyāsiddhaṃ payaḥ pāyayet, tad evāśmantakaśatāvarīpayasyāsiddhaṃ vidārigandhādisiddhaṃ vā, bṛhatīdvayotpalaśatāvarīsārivāpayasyāmadhukasiddhaṃ vā, evaṃ kṣipram upakrāntāyā upāvartante rujo garbhaś cāpy āyate; vyavasthite ca garbhe gavyenoḍumbaraśalāṭusiddhena payasā bhojayet ; atīte lavaṇasnehavarjyābhir yavāgūbhir uddālakādīnāṃ pācanīyopasaṃskṛtābhir upakrameta yāvanto māsā garbhasya tāvanty ahāni; bastyudaraśūleṣu purāṇaguḍaṃ dīpanīyasaṃyuktaṃ pāyayed ariṣṭaṃ vā; vātopadravagṛhītatvāt srotasāṃ līyate garbhaḥ, so 'tikālam avatiṣṭhamāno vyāpadyate, tāṃ mṛdunā snehādikrameṇopacaret, utkrośarasasaṃśsiddhām analpasnehāṃ yavāgūṃ pāyayet, māṣatilabilvaśalāṭusiddhān vā kulmāṣān bhakṣayen madhumādhvīkaṃ cānupibet saptarātraṃ; kālātītasthāyini garbhe viśeṣataḥ sadhānyam udūkhalaṃ musalenābhihanyād viṣame vā yān āsane seveta; vātābhipanna eva śuṣyati garbhaḥ, sa mātuḥ kukṣiṃ na pūrayati mandaṃ spandate ca, taṃ bṛṃhaṇīyaiḥ payobhir māṃsarasaiś copacaret; śukraśoṇitaṃ vāyunā 'bhiprapannam avakrāntajīvam ādhmāpayaty udaraṃ, taṃ kadācid yadṛcchayopaśāntaṃ naigameṣāpahṛtam iti bhāṣante, tam eva kadācit pralīyamānaṃ nāgodaram ity āhuḥ; tatrāpi līnavat pratīkāraḥ / /
SSu.3.10.58/ ata ūrdhvaṃ māsānumāsikaṃ vakṣyāmaḥ / /
SSu.3.10.59/ madhukaṃ śākabījaṃ ca payasyā suradāru ca / / aśmantakas tilāḥ kṛṣṇās tāmravallī śatāvarī / /
SSu.3.10.60/ vṛkṣādanī payasyā ca latā sotpalasārivā / / anantā sārivā rāsnā padmā madhukam eva ca / /
SSu.3.10.61/ bṛhatyau kāśmarī cāpi kṣīriśuṅgās tvaco ghṛtam / / pṛśniparṇī balā śigru śvadaṃṣṭrā madhuparṇikā / /
SSu.3.10.62/ śṛṅgāṭakaṃ bisaṃ drākṣā kaśeru madhukaṃ sitā / / vatsaite sapta yogāḥ syur ardhaślokasamāpanāḥ / / yathāsaṃkhyaṃ prayoktavyā garbhasrāve payoyutāḥ / /
SSu.3.10.63/ kapitthabṛhatībilvapaṭolekṣunidigdhikā- / / mūlāni kṣīrasiddhāni pāyayed bhiṣag aṣṭame / /
SSu.3.10.64/ navame madhukānantāpayasyāsārivāḥ pibet / / kṣīraṃ śuṇṭhīpayasyābhyāṃ siddhaṃ syād daśame hitam / /
SSu.3.10.65/ sakṣīrā vā hitā śuṇṭhī madhukaṃ suradāru ca / / evam āpyāyate garbhas tīvrā ruk copaśāmyati / /
SSu.3.10.66/ nivṛttaprasavāyās tu punaḥ ṣaḍbhyo varṣebhya ūrdhvaṃ prasavam ānāyā nāryāḥ kumāro 'lpāyur bhavati / /
SSu.3.10.67/ atha garbhiṇīṃ vyādhyutpattāv atyaye chardayen madhurāmlenānnopahitenānulomayec ca, saṃśamanīyaṃ ca mṛdu vidadhyād annapānayoḥ, aśnīyāc ca madhuraprāyaṃ garbhāviruddhaṃ ca, garbhāviruddhāś ca yathāyogaṃ vidadhīta mṛdu prāyāḥ / /
SSu.3.10.68/ bhavanti cātra / sauvarṇaṃ sukṛtaṃ cūrṇaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ madhu ghṛtaṃ vacā / / matsyākṣakaḥ śaṅkhapuṣpī madhu sarpiḥ sakāñcanam / /
SSu.3.10.69/ arkapuṣpī madhughṛtaṃ cūrṇitaṃ kanakaṃ vacā / / hemacūrṇāni kaiḍaryaḥ śvetā dūrvā ghṛtaṃ madhu / /
SSu.3.10.70/ catvāro 'bhihitāḥ prāśāḥ ślokārdheṣu caturṣv api / / kumārāṇāṃ vapur medhā balabuddhivivardhanāḥ / /
trailer3.10/ iti suśrutasaṃhitāyāṃ śārīrasthāne garbhiṇīvyākaraṇaṃ śārīraṃ nāma daśamo 'dhyāyaḥ / / 10 / /
trailer3/ samāptaṃ cedaṃ sūtrasthānam /

<B 5>

kalpasthānam
<B 1>

prathamo+adhyāyaḥ/
SSu.5.1.3/ dhanvantariḥ kāśipatistapodharmabhṛtāṃ varaḥ/
SL86/ suśrutaprabhṛtīñchiṣyāñchaśāsāhataśāsanaḥ//
SSu.5.1.4/ ripavo vikramākrāntā ye ca sve kṛtyatāṃ gatāḥ/
SL88/ sisṛkṣavaḥ krodhaviṣaṃ vivaraṃ prāpya tādṛśam//
SSu.5.1.5/ viṣairnihanyurnipuṇaṃ nṛpatiṃ duṣṭacetasaḥ/
SL90/ striyo vā vividhān yogān kadācitsubhagecchayā//
SSu.5.1.6/ viṣakanyopayogādvā kṣaṇājjahyādasūnnaraḥ/
SL92/ tasmādvaidyena satataṃ viṣādrakṣyo narādhipaḥ//
SSu.5.1.7/ yasmācca ceto+anityatvamaśvavat prathitaṃ nṛṇām/
SL94/ na viśvasyāttato rājā kadācidapi kasyacit//
SSu.5.1.8/ kulīnaṃ dhārmikaṃ snigdhaṃ subhṛtaṃ saṃtatotthitam/
SL96/ alubdhamaśaṭhaṃ bhaktaṃ kṛtajñaṃ priyadarśanam//
SSu.5.1.9/ krodhapāruṣyamātsaryamāyālasyavivarjitam/
SL98/ jitendriyaṃ kṣamāvantaṃ śuciṃ śīladayānvitam//
SSu.5.1.10/ medhāvinamasaṃśrāntamanuraktaṃ† hitaiṣiṇam/
SL100/ paṭuṃ pragalbhaṃ nipuṇaṃ dakṣamālasyavarjitam†//
SSu.5.1.11/ pūrvoktaiśca guṇairyuktaṃ nityaṃ sannihitāgadam/
SL102/ mahānase prayuñjīta vaidyaṃ tadvidyapūjitam//
SSu.5.1.12/ praśastadigdeśakṛtaṃ śucibhāṇḍaṃ mahacchuci/
SL104/ sajālakaṃ gavākṣāḍhyamāptavarganiṣevitam//
SSu.5.1.13/ vikakṣasṛṣṭasaṃsṛṣṭaṃ savitānaṃ kṛtārcanam/
SL106/ parīkṣitastrīpuruṣaṃ bhaveccāpi mahānasam//
SSu.5.1.14/ tatrādhyakṣaṃ niyuñjīta prāyo vaidyaguṇānvitam/
SL108/ śucayo dakṣiṇā dakṣā vinītāḥ priyadarśanāḥ//
SSu.5.1.15/ saṃvibhaktāḥ sumanaso nīcakeśanakhāḥ sthirāḥ/
SL110/ snātā dṛḍhaṃ saṃyaminaḥ kṛtoṣṇīṣāḥ susaṃyatāḥ//
SSu.5.1.16/ tasya cājñāvidheyāḥ syurvividhāḥ parikarmiṇaḥ/
SL112/ āhārasthitayaścāpi bhavanti prāṇino yataḥ//
SSu.5.1.17/ tasmānmahānase vaidyaḥ pramādarahito bhavet/
SL114/ māhānasikavoḍhāraḥ saupaudanikapaupikāḥ//
SSu.5.1.18/ bhaveyurvaidyavaśagā ye cāpyanye+atra kecana/
SL116/ iṅgitajño manuṣyāṇāṃ vākceṣṭāmukhavaikṛtaiḥ//
SSu.5.1.19/ vidyādviṣasya dātāramebhirliṅgaiśca buddhimān/
SL118/ na dadātyuttaraṃ pṛṣṭo vivakṣan mohameti ca//
SSu.5.1.20/ apārthaṃ bahu saṅkīrṇaṃ bhāṣate cāpi mūḍhavat/

SL334/ eṣāṃ yavāgūrniṣkvāthe kṛtā hanti viṣadvayam//
SSu.5.2.47/ madhukaṃ tagaraṃ kuṣṭhaṃ bhadradāru hareṇavaḥ/
SL336/ punnāgailailavālūni nāgapuṣpotpalaṃ sitā//
SSu.5.2.48/ viḍaṅgaṃ candanaṃ patraṃ priyaṅgurdhyāmakaṃ tathā/
SL338/ haridre dve bṛhatyau ca sārive ca sthirā sahā//
SSu.5.2.49/ kalkaireṣāṃ ghṛtaṃ siddhamajeyamiti viśrutam/
SL340/ viṣāṇi hanti sarvāṇi śīghramevājitaṃ kvacit//
SSu.5.2.50/ dūṣīviṣārtaṃ susvinnamūrdhvaṃ cādhaśca śodhitam/
SL342/ pāyayetāgadaṃ nityamimaṃ dūṣiviṣāpaham//
SSu.5.2.51/ pippalyo dhyāmakaṃ māṃsī śāvaraḥ paripelavam/
SL344/ suvarcikā sasūkṣmailā toyaṃ kanakagairikam//
SSu.5.2.52/ kṣaudrayukto+agado hyeṣa dūṣīviṣamapohati/
SL346/ nāmnā dūṣīviṣāristu na cānyatrāpi vāryate//
SSu.5.2.53/ jvare dāhe ca hikkāyāmānāhe śukrasaṃkṣaya/
SL348/ śophe+atisāre mūrcchāyāṃ hṛdroge jaṭhare+api ca//
SSu.5.2.54/ unmāde vepathau caiva ye cānye syurupadravāḥ/
SL350/ yathāsvaṃ teṣu kurvīta viṣaghnairauṣadhaiḥ kriyām//
SSu.5.2.55/ sādhyamātmavataḥ sadyo yāpyaṃ savatsarotthitam/
SL352/ dūṣīviṣamasādhyaṃ tu kṣīṇasyāhitasevinaḥ//

<B 3>

tṛtīyo+adhyāyaḥ/
SSu.5.3.3/ jaṅgamasya viṣasyoktānyadhiṣṭhānāni ṣoḍaśa/
SL354/ samāsena mayā yāni vistarasteṣu vakṣyate//
SSu.5.3.6/ bhavanti cātra rājño+arideśe ripavastṛṇāmbumārgānnadhūmaśvasanān viṣeṇa/
SL356/ saṃdūṣayantyebhiratipraduṣṭān vijñāya liṅgairabhiśodhayettān//
SSu.5.3.7/ duṣṭaṃ jalaṃ picchilamugragandhi phenānvitaṃ rājibhirāvṛtaṃ ca/
SL358/ maṇḍūkamatsyaṃ mriyate vihaṅgā mattāśca sānūpacarā bhramanti//
SSu.5.3.8/ majjanti ye cātra narāśvanāgāste chardimohajvaradāhaśophān/
SL360/ ṛcchanti(gacchanti) teṣāmapahṛtya †doṣān duṣṭaṃ jalaṃ śodhayituṃ yateta//
SSu.5.3.9/ dhavāśvakarṇāsanapāribhadrān sapāṭalān siddhakamokṣakau ca/
SL362/ dagdhvā sarājadrumasomavalkāṃstadbhasma śītaṃ vitaret saraḥsu//
SSu.5.3.10/ bhasmāñjaliṃ cāpi ghaṭe nidhāya viśodhayedīpsitamevamambhaḥ/
SL364/ kṣitipradeśaṃ viṣadūṣitaṃ tu †śilātalaṃ tīrthamatheriṇaṃ vā//
SSu.5.3.11/ spṛśanti gātreṇa tu yena yena govājināgoṣṭrakharā narā vā/
SL366/ tacchūnatāṃ yāstyatha dahyate ca viśīryate romanakhaṃ tathaiva//
SSu.5.3.12/ tatrāpyanantāṃ saha sarvagandhaiḥ piṣṭvā surābhirviniyojya mārgam/
SL368/ siñcet payobhiḥ sumṛdanvitaistaṃ viḍaṅgapāṭhākaṭabhījalairvā//
SSu.5.3.13/ tṛṇeṣu bhakteṣu ca dūṣiteṣu sīdanti mūrcchanti vamanti cānye/
SL370/ viḍbhedamṛcchantyathavā mriyante teṣāṃ cikitsāṃ praṇayedyathoktām//
SSu.5.3.14/ viṣāpahairvā+apyagadairvilipya vādyāni citrāṇyapi vādayeta/
SL372/ tāraḥ sutāraḥ sasurendragopaḥ sarvaiśca tulyaḥ kuruvindabhāgaḥ//
SSu.5.3.15/ pittena yuktaḥ kapilānvayena vādyapralepo vihitaḥ praśastaḥ/
SL374/ vādyasya śabdena hi yānti nāśaṃ viṣāṇi ghorāṇyapi yāni †santi//
SSu.5.3.16/ dhūme+anile vā viṣasaṃprayukte khagāḥ śramārtāḥ prapatanti bhūmau/
SL376/ kāsapratiśyāyaśirorujaśca bhavanti tīvrā nayanāmayāśca//
SSu.5.3.17/ lākṣāharidrātiviṣābhayābdahareṇukailādalavakrakuṣṭham/
SL378/ priyaṅgukāṃ cāpyanale nidhāya dhūmānilau cāpi viśodhayeta//
SSu.5.3.18/ prajāmimāmātmayonerbrahmaṇaḥ sṛjataḥ kila/
SL380/ akarodasuro vighnaṃ kaiṭabho nāma darpitaḥ//
SSu.5.3.19/ tasya kruddhasya vai vaktrādbrahmaṇastejaso nidheḥ/
SL382/ krodho vigrahavān bhūtvā nipapātātidāruṇaḥ//
SSu.5.3.20/ sa taṃ dadāha garjantamantakābhaṃ mahābalam/
SL384/ tato+asuraṃ ghātayitvā tattejo+avardhatādbhutam//
SSu.5.3.21/ tato viṣādo devānāmabhavattaṃ nirīkṣya vai/
SL386/ viṣādajananatvācca viṣamityabhidhīyate//
SSu.5.3.22/ tataḥ sṛṣṭvā prajāḥ śeṣaṃ tadā taṃ krodhamīśvaraḥ/
SL388/ vinyastavān sa bhūtesu sthāvareṣu careṣu ca//
SSu.5.3.23/ yathā+avyaktarasaṃ toyamantarīkṣānmahīgatam/
SL390/ teṣu teṣu pradeśeṣu rasaṃ taṃ taṃ niyacchati//
SSu.5.3.24/ evameva viṣaṃ yadyaddravyaṃ vyāpyāvatiṣṭhate/
SL392/ svabhavādeva taṃ tasya rasaṃ samanuvartate//
SSu.5.3.25/ viṣe yasmādguṇāḥ sarve tīkṣṇāḥ prāyeṇa santi hi/
SL394/ viṣaṃ sarvamato jñeyaṃ sarvadoṣaprakopaṇam//
SSu.5.3.26/ te tu vṛttiṃ prakupitā jahati svāṃ viṣārditāḥ/
SL396/ nopayati viṣaṃ pākamataḥ prāṇān ruṇaddhi ca//
SSu.5.3.27/ śleṣmaṇā++āvṛtamārgatvāducchvāso+asya nirudhyate/
SL398/ visaṃjñaḥ sati jīve+api tasmāttiṣṭhati mānavaḥ//
SSu.5.3.28/ śukravat sarvasarpāṇāṃ viṣaṃ sarvaśarīragam/
SL400/ kruddhānāmeti cāṅgebhyaḥ śukraṃ nirmanthanādiva//
SSu.5.3.29/ teṣāṃ baḍiśavaddaṃṣṭrāstāsu sajjati cāgatam/
SL402/ anudvṛttā viṣaṃ tasmānna muñcanti ca bhoginaḥ//
SSu.5.3.30/ yasmādatyarthamuṣṇaṃ ca tīkṣṇaṃ ca paṭhitaṃ viṣam/
SL404/ ataḥ sarvaviṣeṣūktaḥ pariṣekastu śītalaḥ//
SSu.5.3.31/ mandaṃ kīṭeṣu nātyuṣṇaṃ bahuvātakaphaṃ viṣam/
SL406/ ataḥ kīṭaviṣe cāpi svedo na pratiṣidhyate//
SSu.5.3.32/ kīṭairdaṣṭānugraviṣaiḥ sarpavat samupācaret/
SL408/ svabhāvādeva tiṣṭhettu prahārādaṃśayorviṣam//
SSu.5.3.33/ vyāpya sāvayavaṃ dehaṃ digdhaviddhāhidaṣṭayoḥ/
SL410/ laulyādviṣānvitaṃ māṃsaṃ yaḥ khādenmṛtamātrayoḥ//
SSu.5.3.34/ yathāviṣaṃ sa rogeṇa kliśyate mriyate+api vā/
SL412/ ataścāpyanayormāṃsamabhakṣyaṃ mṛtamātrayoḥ//
SSu.5.3.35/ muhūrtāttadupādeyaṃ prahārādaṃśabarjitam/
SL414/ savātaṃ gṛhadhūmābhaṃ purīṣaṃ yo+atisāryate//
SSu.5.3.36/ ādhmāto+atyarthamuṣṇāsro vivarṇaḥ sādapīḍitaḥ/
SL416/ udvamatyatha phenaṃ ca viṣapītaṃ tamādiśet//
SSu.5.3.37/ na cāsya hṛdayaṃ vahnirviṣajuṣṭaṃ dahatyapi/
SL418/ taddhi sthānaṃ cetanāyāḥ svabhavādvyāpya tiṣṭhiti//
SSu.5.3.38/ aśvatthadevāyatanaśmaśānavalmīkasandhyāsu catuṣpatheṣu/
SL420/ yāmye sapitrye parivarjanīyā ṛkṣe narā marmasu ye ca daṣṭāḥ//
SSu.5.3.39/ darvīkarāṇāṃ viṣamāśughāti sarvāṇi coṣṇe dviguṇībhavanti/
SL422/ ajīrṇapittātapapīḍiteṣu bālapramehiṣvatha garbhiṇīṣu//
SSu.5.3.40/ vṛddhāturakṣīṇabubhukṣiteṣu rūkṣeṣu bhīruṣvatha durdineṣu/
SL424/ śastrakṣate yasya na raktameti rājyo latābhiśca na saṃbhavanti//
SSu.5.3.41/ śītābhiradbhiśca na romaharṣo viṣābhibhūtaṃ parivarjayettam/
SL426/ jihvā sitā yasya ca keśaśāto nāsāvabhaṅgaśca sakaṇṭhabhaṅgaḥ//
SSu.5.3.42/ kṛṣṇaḥ saraktaḥ śvayathuśca daṃśe hanvoḥ sthiratvaṃ ca sa varjanīyaḥ/
SL428/ vartirghanā yasya nireti vaktrādraktaṃ sravedūrdhvamadhaśca yasya//
SSu.5.3.43/ daṃṣṭrānipātāḥ sakalāśca yasya taṃ cāpi vaidyaḥ parivarjayettu/
SL430/ unmattamatyarthamupadrutaṃ vā hīnasvaraṃ vā+apyathavā vivarṇam//
SSu.5.3.44/ sāriṣṭamatyarthamaveginaṃ ca jahyānnaraṃ tatra na karma kuryāt//

<B 4>

caturtho+adhyāyaḥ/
SSu.5.4.3/ dhanvantariṃ mahāprājñaṃ sarvaśāstraviśāradam/
SL433/ pādayorupasaṃgṛhya suśrutaḥ paripṛcchati//
SSu.5.4.4/ sarpasaṃkhyāṃ vibhāgaṃ ca daṣṭalakṣaṇameva ca/
SL435/ jñānaṃ ca viṣavegānāṃ bhagavan vaktumarhasi//
SSu.5.4.5/ tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā prābravīdbhiṣajāṃ varaḥ/
SL437/ asaṃkhyā †vāsukiśreṣṭhā vikhyātāstakṣakādayaḥ//
SSu.5.4.6/ mahīdharāśca nāgendrā hutāgnisamatejasaḥ/
SL439/ ye cāpyajasraṃ garjanti varṣanti ca tapanti ca//
SSu.5.4.7/ sasāgaragiridvīpā yairiyaṃ dhāryate mahī/
SL441/ kruddhā niḥśvāsadṛṣṭibhyāṃ ye hanyurakhilaṃ jagat//
SSu.5.4.8/ namastebhyo+asti no teṣāṃ kāryaṃ kiñciccikitsayā/
SL443/ ye tu daṃṣṭrāviṣā bhaumā ye daśanti ca mānuṣān//
SSu.5.4.9/ teṣāṃ saṅkhyāṃ pravakṣyāmi yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ/
SL445/ aśītistveva sarpāṇāṃ bhidyate pañcadhā tu sā//
SSu.5.4.10/ darvīkarā maṇḍalino rājimantastathaiva ca/
SL447/ nirviṣā vaikarañjāśca trividhāste punaḥ smṛtāḥ//
SSu.5.4.11/ darvīkarā maṇḍalino rājimantaśca †pannagāḥ/
SL449/ teṣu darvīkarā jñeyā viṃśatiḥ ṣaṭ ca pannagāḥ//
SSu.5.4.12/ dvāviṃśatirmaṇḍalino rājimantastathā daśa/
SL451/ nirviṣā dvādaśa jñeyā vaikarañjāstrayastathā//
SSu.5.4.13/ vaikarañjodbhavāḥ sapta citrā† maṇḍalirājilāḥ/
SL453/ pādābhimṛṣṭā duṣṭā vā kruddhā grāsārthino+api vā//
SSu.5.4.14/ te daśanti mahākrodhāstrividhaṃ† bhīmadarśanāḥ/
SL455/ sarpitaṃ raditaṃ cāpi tṛtīyamatha nirviṣam/
SL456/ sarpāṅgābhihataṃ kecidicchanti khalu tadvidaḥ//
SSu.5.4.15/ padāni yatra dantānāmekaṃ dve vā bahūni vā/
SL458/ nimagnānyalparaktāni yānyudvṛtya karoti hi//
SSu.5.4.16/ cañcumālakayuktāni vaikṛtyakaraṇāni ca/
SL460/ saṅkṣiptāni saśophāni vidyāttat sarpitaṃ bhiṣak//
SSu.5.4.17/ rājyaḥ salohitā yatra nīlāḥ pītāḥ sitāstathā/
SL462/ vijñeyaṃ raditaṃ †tattu jñeyamalpaviṣaṃ ca tat//
SSu.5.4.18/ aśophamalpaduṣṭāsṛk prakṛtisthasya dehinaḥ/
SL464/ padaṃ padāni vā vidyādaviṣaṃ taccikitsakaḥ//
SSu.5.4.19/ sarpaspṛṣṭasya bhīrorhi bhayena kupito+anilaḥ/
SL466/ kasyacit kurute śophaṃ sarpāṅgābhihataṃ tu tat//
SSu.5.4.20/ vyādhitodvignadaṣṭāni jñeyānyalpaviṣāṇi tu/
SL468/ tathā+ativṛddhabālābhidaṣṭamalpaviṣaṃ smṛtam//
SSu.5.4.21/ suparṇadevabrahmarṣiyakṣasiddhaniṣevite/
SL470/ viṣaghnauṣadhiyukte ca deśe na kramate viṣam//
SSu.5.4.22/ rathāṅgalāṅgalacchatrasvastikāṅkuśadhāriṇaḥ/
SL472/ jñeyā darvīkarāḥ sarpāḥ phaṇinaḥ śīghragāminaḥ//
SSu.5.4.23/ maṇḍalairvividhaiścitrāḥ pṛthavo mandagāminaḥ/
SL474/ jñeyā maṇḍalinaḥ sarpā †jvalanārkasamaprabhāḥ//
SSu.5.4.24/ snigdhā vividhavarṇābhistiryagūrdhvaṃ ca rājibhiḥ/
SL476/ citritā iva ye bhānti rājimantastu te smṛtāḥ//
SSu.5.4.25/ muktārūpyaprabhā ye ca kapilā ye ca pannagāḥ/
SL478/ sugandhayaḥ suvarṇābhāste jātyā brāhmaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ//
SSu.5.4.26/ kṣatriyāḥ snigdhavarṇāstu pannagā bhṛśakopanāḥ/
SL480/ sūryacandrākṛticchatralakṣma teṣāṃ tathā+ambujam//
SSu.5.4.27/ kṛṣṇā †vajranibhā ye ca lohitā varṇatastathā/
SL482/ dhūmrāḥ pārāvatābhāśca vaiśyāste pannagāḥ smṛtāḥ//
SSu.5.4.28/ mahiṣadvīpivarṇābhāstathaiva paruṣatvacaḥ/
SL484/ bhinnavarṇāśca ye kecicchūdrāte parikīrtitāḥ//
SSu.5.4.29/ kopayantyanilaṃ jantoḥ phaṇinaḥ sarva eva tu/
SL486/ pittaṃ maṇḍalinaścāpi kaphaṃ cānekarājayaḥ//
SSu.5.4.30/ apatyamasavarṇābhyāṃ dvidoṣakaralakṣaṇam/
SL488/ jñeyau doṣaiśca dampatyorviśeṣaścātra vakṣyate//
SSu.5.4.31/ rajanyāḥ paścime yāme sarpāścitrāścaranti hi/
SL490/ śeṣeṣūktā maṇḍalino divā darvīkarāḥ smṛtāḥ//
SSu.5.4.32/ darvīkarāstu taruṇā vṛddhā maṇḍalinastathā/
SL492/ rājimanto vayomadhyā jāyante mṛtyuhetavaḥ//
SSu.5.4.33/ nakulākulitā bālā vāriviprahatāḥ kṛśāḥ/
SL494/ vṛddhāmuktatvaco bhītāḥ sarpāstvalpaviṣāḥ smṛtāḥ//
SSu.5.4.40/ bhavanti cātra dhātvantareṣu yāḥ sapta kalāḥ saṃparikīrtitāḥ/
SL496/ tāsvekaikāmatikramya vegaṃ prakurute viṣam//
SSu.5.4.41/ yenāntareṇa tu kalāṃ kālakalpaṃ bhinatti hi/
SL498/ samīraṇenohyamānaṃ tattu vegāntaraṃ smṛtam//
SSu.5.4.42/ śūnāṅgaḥ prathame vege paśurdhyāyati duḥkhitaḥ/
SL500/ lālāsrāvo dvitīye tu kṛṣṇāṅgaḥ† pīḍyate hṛdi//
SSu.5.4.43/ tṛtīye ca śiroduḥkhaṃ kaṇṭhagrīvaṃ ca bhajyate/
SL502/ caturthe vepate mūḍhaḥ khādan dantān jahātyasūn//
SSu.5.4.44/ kecidvegatrayaṃ prāhurantaṃ caiteṣu tadvidaḥ/
SL504/ dhyāyati prathame vege pakṣī muhyatyataḥ param//
SSu.5.4.45/ dvitīye vihvalaḥ proktastṛtīye† mṛtyumṛcchati/
SL506/ kecidekaṃ vihaṅgeṣu viṣavegamuśanti hi/
SL507/ mārjāranakulādīnāṃ viṣaṃ †nātipravartate//

<B 5>

pañcamo+adhyāyaḥ/
SSu.5.5.3/ sarvairevāditaḥ sarpaiḥ śākhādaṣṭasya dehinaḥ/
SL509/ daṃśasyopati badhnīyādariṣṭāścaturaṅgule//
SSu.5.5.4/ plotacarmāntavalkānāṃ mṛdunā+anyatamena vai/
SL511/ na gacchati viṣaṃ dehamariṣṭābhirnivāritam//
SSu.5.5.5/ daheddaṃśamathotkṛtya yatra bandho na jāyate/
SL513/ ācūṣaṇacchedadāhāḥ sarvatraiva tu pūjitāḥ//
SSu.5.5.6/ pratipūrya mukhaṃ vastrairhitamācūṣaṇaṃ bhavet/
SL515/ sa †daṣṭavyo+athavā sarpo loṣṭo vā+api hi tatkṣaṇam//
SSu.5.5.7/ atha maṇḍalinā daṣṭaṃ na kathaṃcana dāhayet/
SL517/ sa pittabāhulyaviṣāddaṃśo dāhādvisarpate//
SSu.5.5.8/ ariṣṭāmapi mantraiśca badhnīyānmantrakovidaḥ/
SL519/ sā tu rajjvādibhirbaddhā viṣapratikarī matā//
SSu.5.5.9/ devabrahmarṣibhiḥ proktā mantrāḥ satyatapomayāḥ/
SL521/ bhavanti nānyathā kṣipraṃ viṣaṃ hanyuḥ sudustaram//
SSu.5.5.10/ viṣaṃ tejomayairmantraiḥ satyabrahmatapomayaiḥ/
SL523/ yathā nivāryate kṣipraṃ prayuktairna tathauṣadhaiḥ//
SSu.5.5.11/ mantrāṇāṃ grahaṇaṃ kāryaṃ strīmāṃsamadhuvarjinā/
SL525/ †mitāhāreṇa śucinā kuśāstaraṇaśāyinā//
SSu.5.5.12/ gandhamālyopahāraiśca balibhiścāpi devatāḥ/
SL527/ pūjayenmantrasiddhyarthaṃ japahomaiśca yatnataḥ//
SSu.5.5.13/ mantrāstvavidhinā proktā hīnā vā svaravarṇataḥ/
SL529/ yasmānna siddhimāyānti tasmādyojyo+agadakramaḥ//
SSu.5.5.14/ samantataḥ sirā daṃśādvidhyettu kuśalo bhiṣak/
SL531/ śākhāgre vā lalāṭe vā vyadhyāstā viṛte viṣe//
SSu.5.5.15/ rakte nirhriyamāṇe tu kṛtsnaṃ nirhriyate viṣam/
SL533/ tasmādvisrāvayedraktaṃ sā hyasya paramā kriyā//
SSu.5.5.16/ samantādagadairdaṃśaṃ pracchayitvā pralepayet/
SL535/ candanośīrayuktena vāriṇā pariṣecayet//
SSu.5.5.17/ pāyayetāgadāṃstāṃstān kṣīrakṣaudraghṛtādibhiḥ/
SL537/ tadalābhe hitā vā syāt kṛṣṇā valmīkamṛttikā//
SSu.5.5.18/ kovidāraśirīṣārkakaṭabhīrvā+api bhakṣayet/
SL539/ na pibettailakaulatthamadyasauvīrakāṇi ca//
SSu.5.5.19/ dravamanyattu yatkiñcit pītvā pītvā tadudvamet/
SL541/ prāyo hi vamanenaiva mukhaṃ nirhriyate viṣam//
SSu.5.5.20/ phaṇināṃ viṣavege tu prathame śoṇitaṃ haret/
SL543/ dvitīye madhusarpirbhyāṃ pāyayetāgadaṃ bhisak//
SSu.5.5.21/ nasyakarmāñjane yuñjyāttṛtīye viṣanāśane/
SL545/ vāntaṃ caturthe pūrvoktāṃ yavāgūmatha dāpayet//
SSu.5.5.22/ śītopacāraṃ kṛtvā++ādau bhiṣak pañcamaṣaṣṭhayoḥ/
SL547/ †pāyayecchodhanaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ yavāgūṃ cāpi kīrtitām//
SSu.5.5.23/ saptame tvavapīḍena śirastīkṣṇena śodhayet/
SL549/ tīkṣṇamevāñjanaṃ dadyāt tīkṣṇaśastreṇa mūrdhni ca//
SSu.5.5.24/ kṛtvā kākapadaṃ carma sāsṛgvā piśitaṃ† kṣipet/
SL551/ pūrve maṇḍalināṃ vege darvīkaravadācaret//
SSu.5.5.25/ agadaṃ madhusarpirbhyāṃ dvitīye pāyayeta ca/
SL553/ vāmayitvā yavāgūṃ ca pūrvoktāmatha dāpayet//
SSu.5.5.26/ tṛtīye śodhitaṃ tīkṣṇairvāgūṃ pāyayeddhitām/
SL555/ caturthe pañcame cāpi darvīkaravadācaret//
SSu.5.5.27/ kākolyādirhitaḥ ṣaṣṭhe peyaśca †madhuro+agadaḥ/
SL557/ hito+avapīḍe tvagadaḥ saptame viṣanāśanaḥ//
SSu.5.5.28/ pūrve rājimatāṃ vege+alābudhiḥ śoṇitaṃ haret†/
SL559/ agadaṃ madhusarpirbhyāṃ saṃyuktaṃ pāyayeta ca//
SSu.5.5.29/ vāntaṃ dvitīye tvagadaṃ pāyayedviṣanāśanam/
SL561/ tṛtīyādiṣu triṣvevaṃ vidhirdārvīkaro hitaḥ//
SSu.5.5.30/ ṣaṣṭhe+añjanaṃ tīkṣṇatamamavapīḍaśca saptame/
SL563/ garbhiṇībālavṛddhānāṃ sirāvyadhanavarjitam//
SSu.5.5.31/ viṣārtānāṃ yathoddiṣṭaṃ vidhānaṃ śasyate mṛdu/
SL565/ raktāvasekāñjanāni naratulyānyajāvike//
SSu.5.5.32/ triguṇaṃ mahiṣe soṣṭre gavāśve dviguṇaṃ tu tat†/
SL567/ caturguṇaṃ tu nāgānāṃ kevalaṃ sarvapakṣiṇām//
SSu.5.5.33/ pariṣekān pradehāṃśca suśītānavacārayet/
SL569/ māṣakaṃ tvañjanasyeṣṭaṃ dviguṇaṃ nasyato hitam/
SL570/ pāne caturguṇaṃ pathyaṃ vamane+aṣṭaguṇaṃ punaḥ//
SSu.5.5.34/ deśaprakṛtisātmyartuviṣavegabalābalam/
SL572/ pradhārya nipuṇaṃ buddhyā tataḥ karma samācaret//
SSu.5.5.35/ vegānupūrvyā karmoktamidaṃ viṣavināśanam†/
SL574/ karmāvasthāviśeṣeṇa viṣayorubhayoḥ śṛṇu//
SSu.5.5.36/ vivarṇe kaṭhine śūne saruje+aṅge †viṣānvite/
SL576/ tūrṇaṃ visravaṇaṃ kāryamuktena vidhinā tataḥ//
SSu.5.5.37/ kṣudhārtamanilaprāyaṃ tadviṣārtaṃ† samāhitaḥ/
SL578/ pāyayeta rasaṃ sarpiḥ †śuktaṃ kṣaudraṃ tathā dadhi//
SSu.5.5.38/ tṛḍdāhadharmasaṃmohe paittaṃ paittaviṣāturam†/
SL580/ śītaiḥ saṃvāhanasnānapradehaiḥ samupācaret//
SSu.5.5.39/ śīte śītaprasekārtaṃ ślaiṣmikaṃ kaphakṛdviṣam/
SL582/ vāmayedvamanaistīkṣṇaistathā mūrcchāmadānvitam//
SSu.5.5.40/ koṣṭhadāharujādhmānamūtrasaṅgaruganvitam/
SL584/ virecayecchakṛdvāyusaṅgapittāturaṃ naram//
SSu.5.5.41/ śūnākṣikūṭaṃ nidrārtaṃ vivarṇāvilalocanam/
SL586/ vivarṇaṃ cāpi paśyantamañjanaiḥ samupācaret//
SSu.5.5.42/ śiroruggauravālasyahanustambhagalagrahe/
SL588/ śiro virecayet kṣipraṃ manyāstambhe ca dāruṇe//
SSu.5.5.43/ naṣṭasaṃjñaṃ vivṛttākṣaṃ bhagnagrīvaṃ virecanaiḥ/
SL590/ cūrṇaiḥ pradhamanaistīkṣṇairviṣārtaṃ samupācaret//
SSu.5.5.44/ tāḍayecca sirāḥ kṣipraṃ tasya śākhālalāṭajāḥ/
SL592/ tāsvaprasicyamānāsu mūrdhni śastreṇa śastravit//
SSu.5.5.45/ kuryāt kākapadākāraṃ vraṇamevaṃ sravanti tāḥ/
SL594/ saraktaṃ carma māṃsaṃ vā nikṣipeccāsya mūrdhani//
SSu.5.5.46/ carmavṛkṣakaṣāyaṃ vā †kalkaṃ vā kuśalo bhiṣak/
SL596/ vādayeccāgadairliptvā dundubhīṃstasya pārśvayoḥ//
SSu.5.5.47/ labdhasaṃjñaṃ punaścainamūrdhvaṃ cādhaśca śodhayet/
SL598/ niḥśeṣaṃ nirhareccaivaṃ viṣaṃ paramadurjayam//
SSu.5.5.48/ alpamapyavaśiṣṭaṃ hi bhūyo vegāya kalpate/
SL600/ kuryādvā sādavaivarṇyajvarakāsaśirorujaḥ//
SSu.5.5.49/ śophaśoṣapratiśyāyatimirārucipīnasān/
SL602/ teṣu cāpi yathādoṣaṃ pratikarma prayojayet//
SSu.5.5.50/ viṣārtopadravāṃścāpi yathāsvaṃ samupācaret/
SL604/ athāriṣṭāṃ vimucyāśu† pracchayitvā+aṅkitaṃ tayā†//
SSu.5.5.51/ dahyāttatra viṣaṃ skannaṃ bhūyo vegāya kalpate/
SL606/ evamauṣadhibhirmantraiḥ kriyāyogaiśca yatnataḥ//
SSu.5.5.52/ viṣe hṛtaguṇe dehādyadā doṣaḥ prakupyati/
SL608/ tadā pavanamudvṛttaṃ snehādyaiḥ samupācaret//
SSu.5.5.53/ tailamatsyakulatthāmlavarjyairviṣaharāyutaiḥ†/
SL610/ pittajvraharaiḥ pittaṃ kaṣāyasnehabastibhiḥ//
SSu.5.5.54/ kaphamāragvadhādyena sakṣaudreṇa gaṇena tu/
SL612/ śleṣmaghnairagadaiścaiva tiktai rūkṣaiśca bhojanaiḥ//
SSu.5.5.55/ vṛkṣaprapātaviṣamapatitaṃ mṛtamambhasi/
SL614/ udbaddhaṃ ca mṛtaṃ sadyaścikitsennaṣṭasaṃjñavat//
SSu.5.5.56/ gāḍhaṃ baddhe+ariṣṭayā pracchite vā tīkṣṇairlepaistadvidhairvā+†avaśiṣṭaiḥ/
SL616/ śūne gātre klinnamatyarthapūti jñeyaṃ māṃsaṃ tadviṣāt pūti kaṣṭam//
SSu.5.5.57/ sadyo viddhaṃ nisravet kṛṣṇaraktaṃ pākaṃ yāyāddahyate cāpyabhīkṣṇam/
SL618/ kṛṣṇībhūtaṃ klinnamatyarthapūti śīrṇaṃ māṃsaṃ yātyajasraṃ kṣatācca//
SSu.5.5.58/ tṛṣṇā mūrcchā bhrāntidāhau jvaraśca yasya syustaṃ digdhaviddhaṃ vyavasyet/
SL620/ pūrvoddiṣṭaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ sarvametajjuṣṭaṃ yasyālaṃ viṣeṇa vraṇāḥ syuḥ//
SSu.5.5.59/ lūtādaṣṭā digdhaviddhā viṣairvā juṣṭā prāyaste vraṇāḥ pūtimāṃsāḥ†/
SL622/ teṣāṃ yuktyā pūtimāṃsānyapohya vāryokobhiḥ śoṇitaṃ cāpahṛtya//
SSu.5.5.60/ hṛtvā doṣān kṣipramūrdhvaṃ tvadhaśca samyak siñcet kṣīriṇāṃ tvakkaṣāyaiḥ/
SL624/ antarvastraṃ dāpayecca pradehān śītairdravyairājyayuktairviṣaghnaiḥ//
SSu.5.5.61/ bhinne tvasthnā duṣṭajātena kāryaḥ pūrvo mārgaḥ paittike yo viṣe ca/
SL626/ trivṛdviśalye madhukaṃ haridre raktā narendro lavaṇaśca vargaḥ//
SSu.5.5.62/ kaṭutrikaṃ caiva sucūrṇitāni śṛṅge nidadhyānmadhusaṃyutāni/
SL628/ eṣo+agado hanti viṣaṃ prayuktaḥ pānāñjanābhyañjananasyayogaiḥ//
SSu.5.5.63/ avāryavīryo viṣavegahantā† mahāgado nāma mahāprabhāvaḥ/
SL630/ viḍaṅgapāṭhātriphalājamodāhiṅgūni vakraṃ trikaṭūni caiva//
SSu.5.5.64/ sarvaśca vargo lavaṇaḥ susūkṣmaḥ sacitrakaḥ kṣaudrayuto nidheyaḥ/
SL632/ śṛṅge gavāṃ śṛṅgamayena caiva pracchāditaḥ pakṣamupekṣitaśca//
SSu.5.5.65/ eṣo+agadaḥ sthāvarajaṅgamānāṃ jetā† viṣāṇāmajito hi nāmnā/
SL634/ prapauṇḍarīkaṃ suradāru mustā kālānusāryā kaṭurohiṇī ca//
SSu.5.5.66/ sthauṇeyakadhyāmakaguggulūni† punnāgatālīśasuvarcikāśca/
SL636/ kuṭannaṭailāsitasindhuvārāḥ śaileyakuṣṭhe tagaraṃ priyaṅguḥ//
SSu.5.5.67/ rodhraṃ jalaṃ kāñcanagairikaṃ ca samāgadhaṃ candanasaindhavaṃ ca/
SL638/ sūkṣmāṇi cūrṇāni samāni kṛtvā śṛṅge nidadhyānmadhusaṃyutāni//
SSu.5.5.68/ eṣo+agadastārkṣya iti pradiṣṭo viṣaṃ nihanyādapi takṣakasya/
SL640/ māṃsīhareṇutriphalāmuraṅgīraktālatāyaṣṭikapadmakāni//

SL646/ yasyāgado+ayaṃ sukṛto gṛhe syānnamnarṣabho nāma nararṣabhasya//
SSu.5.5.72/ na tatra sarpāḥ kuta eva kīṭāstyajanti vīryāṇi viṣāṇi caiva/
SL648/ etena bheryaḥ paṭahāśca digdhā nānadyamānā viṣamāśu hanyuḥ//

SSu.5.5.75/ saṃjīvano nāma gatāsukalpāneṣo+agado jīvayatīha martyān/

SSu.5.5.76/ sataṇḍulīyo+agada eṣa mukhyo viṣeṣu darvīkararājilānām/

SSu.5.5.77/ deyo dvibhāgaḥ surasācchadasya kapitthabilvādapi dāḍimācca/

SSu.5.5.78/ eṣo+agadaḥ kṣaudrayuto nihanti viśeṣato maṇḍalināṃ viṣāṇi/
SL660/ vaṃśatvagārdrā++āmalakaṃ kapitthaṃ kaṭutrikaṃ haimavatī sakuṣṭhā//
SSu.5.5.79/ karañjabījaṃ tagaraṃ śirīṣapuṣpaṃ ca gopittayutaṃ nihanti/
SL662/ viṣāṇi lūtondurapannagānāṃ kaiṭaṃ ca lepāñjananasyapānaiḥ//
SSu.5.5.80/ pūrīṣamūtrānilagarbhasaṅgānnihanti vartyañjananābhilepaiḥ/
SL664/ kācārmakothān paṭalāṃśca ghorān puṣpaṃ ca hantyañjananasyayogaiḥ//
SSu.5.5.81/ samūlapuṣpāṅkuravalkabījāt kvāthaḥ śirīṣāt trikaṭupragāḍhaḥ/
SL666/ salāvaṇaḥ kṣaudrayuto+atha pīto viśeṣataḥ kīṭaviṣaṃ nihanti//
SSu.5.5.82/ kuṣṭhaṃ trikaṭukaṃ dārvī madhukaṃ lavaṇadvayam/
SL668/ mālatī nāgapuṣpaṃ ca sarvāṇi madhurāṇi ca//
SSu.5.5.83/ kapittharasapiṣṭo+ayaṃ śarkarākṣaudrasaṃyutaḥ/

SL672/ corako varuṇaḥ kuṣṭhaṃ sarpagandhā sasaptalā//
SSu.5.5.85/ punarnavā śirīṣasya puṣpamāragvadhārkajam/

ṣaṣṭho+adhyāyaḥ/
SSu.5.6.4/ anena dundubhiṃ limpet patākāṃ toraṇāni ca/

SL706/ somarājīmativiṣāṃ pṛthvikāmindravāruṇīm†//
SSu.5.6.19/ uśīraṃ varuṇaṃ mustaṃ kustumburu nakhaṃ tathā/

SSu.5.6.22/ dhavāśvakarṇapārthānāṃ puṣpāṇi tiniśasya ca/
SL714/ gugguluṃ kuṅkumaṃ bimbīṃ sarpākṣīṃ gandhanākulīm//

SL734/ †prasannavarṇendriyacittaceṣṭaṃ vaidyo+avagacchedaviṣaṃ manuṣyam//

<B 7>

saptamo+adhyāyaḥ/
SSu.5.7.3/ pūrvaṃ śukraviṣā uktā bhūṣikā ye samāsataḥ/
SL736/ nāmalakṣaṇabhaiṣajyairaṣṭādaśa nibodha me†//
SSu.5.7.4/ lālanaḥ putrakaḥ kṛṣṇo haṃsiraścikviras(haṃsiraścikkiras) tathā/
SL738/ chucchundaro+alasaścaiva kaṣāyadaśano+api ca//
SSu.5.7.5/ kuliṅgaścājitaścaiva capalaḥ kapilastathā/
SL740/ kokilo+aruṇasaṃjñaśca mahākṛṣṇastathonduraḥ//
SSu.5.7.6/ śvetena mahatā sārdhaṃ kapilenākhunā tathā/
SL742/ mūṣikaśca kapotābhastathaivāṣṭādaśa smṛtāḥ//
SSu.5.7.7/ śukraṃ patati yatraiṣāṃ śukraspṛṣṭaiḥ† spṛśanti vā/
SL744/ nakhadantādibhistasmin gātre raktaṃ praduṣyati//
SSu.5.7.8/ jāyante granthayaḥ śophāḥ karṇikā maṇḍalāni ca/

SSu.5.8.45/ kṣīramajjavasāsarpiḥśuṇṭhīpippalidāruṣu/
SL937/ utkārikā sthirādau vā sukṛtā svedane hitā//
SSu.5.8.46/ na svedayeta cādaṃśaṃ dhūmaṃ vakṣyāmi vṛścike/
SL939/ agadānekajātīṣu pravakṣyāmi pṛthak pṛthak//
SSu.5.8.47/ kuṣṭhaṃ vakraṃ vacā pāṭhā bilvamūlaṃ suvarcikā/
SL941/ gṛhadhūmaṃ haridre dve trikaṇṭakaviṣe hitāḥ//
SSu.5.8.48/ rajanyāgāradhūmaśca vakraṃ kuṣṭhaṃ palāśajam/
SL943/ galagolikadaṣṭānāmagado viṣanāśanaḥ//
SSu.5.8.49/ kuṅkumaṃ tagaraṃ śigru padmakaṃ rajanīdvayam/
SL945/ agado jalapiṣṭo+ayaṃ śatapadviṣanāśanaḥ//
SSu.5.8.50/ meṣaśṛṅgī vacā pāṭhā niculo rohiṇī jalam/
SL947/ sarvamaṇḍūkadaṣṭānāmagado+ayaṃ viṣāpahaḥ//
SSu.5.8.51/ †dhavāśvagandhātibalābalāsātiguhāguhāḥ/
SL949/ viśvambharābhidaṣṭānāmagado+ayaṃ viṣāpahaḥ//
SSu.5.8.52/ śirīṣaṃ tagaraṃ kuṣṭhaṃ śāliparṇī sahā niśe/
SL951/ ahiṇḍukābhirdaṣṭānāmagado viṣanāśanaḥ//
SSu.5.8.53/ kaṇḍūmakābhirdaṣṭānāṃ rātrau śītāḥ kriyā hitāḥ/
SL953/ divā te naiva sidhyanti sūryaraśmibalārditāḥ//
SSu.5.8.54/ vakraṃ kuṣṭhamapāmārgaḥ śūkavṛntaviṣe+agadaḥ/
SL955/ bhṛṅgasvarasapiṣṭā vā kṛṣṇavalmīkamṛttikā//
SSu.5.8.55/ pipīlikābhirdaṣṭānāṃ makṣikāmaśakaistathā/
SL957/ gomūtreṇa yuto lepaḥ kṛṣṇavalmīkamṛttikā//
SSu.5.8.56/ nakhāvaghṛṣṭasaṃjāte śophe bhṛṅgaraso hitaḥ†/
SL959/ pratisūryakadaṣṭānāṃ sarpadaṣṭavadāceret/
SL960/ trividhā vṛścikāḥ proktā mandamadhyamahāviṣāḥ//
SSu.5.8.57/ gośakṛtkothajā mandā madhyāḥ kāṣṭheṣṭikodbhavāḥ/
SL962/ sarpakothodbhavāstīkṣṇā ye cānye viṣasaṃbhavāḥ//
SSu.5.8.58/ mandā dvādaśa madhyāstu trayaḥ pañcadaśottamāḥ/
SL964/ daśa viṃśatirityete saṃkhyayā parikīrtitāḥ//
SSu.5.8.59/ kṛṣṇaḥ śyāvaḥ karburaḥ †pāṇḍuvarṇo gomūtrābhaḥ †karkaśo mecakaśca/
SL966/ †pīto dhūmro romaśaḥ śāḍvalābho raktaḥ śvetenodareṇeti mandāḥ†//
SSu.5.8.60/ yuktāścaite vṛścikāḥ pucchadeśe syurbhūyobhiḥ parvabhiścetarebhyaḥ/
SL968/ ebhirdaṣṭe vedanā vepathuśca gātrastambhaḥ kṛṣṇaraktāgamaśca//
SSu.5.8.61/ śākhādaṣṭe vedanā cordhvameti dāhasvedau daṃśaśopho jvaraśca/
SL970/ raktaḥ pītaḥ kāpilenodareṇa sarve dhūmrāḥ parvabhiśca tribhiḥ syuḥ//
SSu.5.8.62/ ete mūtroccārapūtyaṇḍajātā madhyā jñeyāstriprakāroragāṇām/
SL972/ yasyaiteṣāmanvayādyaḥ prasūto doṣotpattiṃ tatsvarūpāṃ sa kuryāt//
SSu.5.8.63/ jihvāśopho †bhojanasyāvarodho mūrcchā cogrā madhyavīryābhidaṣṭe†/
SL974/ śvetaścitraḥ śyāmalo lohitābho† raktaḥ śveto raktanīlodarau ca//
SSu.5.8.64/ †pīto+arakto nīlapīto+aparastu rakto nīlo nīlaśuklastathā ca/
SL976/ rakto babhruḥ pūrvavaccaikaparvā yaścāparvā parvaṇī dve ca yasya//
SSu.5.8.65/ nānārūpā varṇataścāpi ghorā jñeyāścaite vṛścikāḥ prāṇacaurāḥ†/
SL978/ janmaiteṣāṃ sarpakothāt pradiṣṭaṃ dehebhyo vā ghātitānāṃ viṣeṇa//
SSu.5.8.66/ ebhirdaṣṭe sarpavegapravṛttiḥ sphoṭotpattirbhrāntidāhau jvaraśca/
SL980/ khebhyaḥ kṛṣṇaṃ śoṇitaṃ yāti tīvraṃ tasmāt† prāṇaistyajyate śīghrameva//
SSu.5.8.67/ ugramadhyaviṣairdaṣṭaṃ cikitset sarpadaṣṭavat/
SL982/ ādaṃśaṃ sveditaṃ cūrṇaiḥ pracchitaṃ pratisārayet//
SSu.5.8.68/ rajanīsaindhavavyoṣaśirīṣaphalapuṣpajaiḥ/
SL984/ mātuluṅgāmlagomūtrapiṣṭaṃ ca surasāgrajam//
SSu.5.8.69/ lepe svede sukhoṣṇaṃ ca gomayaṃ hitamiṣyate/

SSu.5.8.70/ daṃśaṃ mandaviṣāṇāṃ tu cakratailena secayet/

SSu.5.8.71/ kuryāccotkārikāsvedaṃ viṣaghnairupanāhayet†/

SSu.5.8.73/ dhūmo hanti prayuktastu śīghraṃ vṛścikajaṃ viṣam/
SL994/ kusumbhapuṣpaṃ rajanī niśā vā kodravaṃ tṛṇam//
SSu.5.8.74/ ebhirghṛtāktairdhūpastu pāyudeśe prayojitaḥ/
SL996/ nāśayedāśu kīṭotthaṃ vṛścikasya ca yadviṣam//
SSu.5.8.75/ lūtāviṣaṃ ghoratamaṃ durvijñeyatamaṃ ca tat/
SL998/ †duścikitsyatamaṃ cāpi bhiṣagbhirmandabuddhibhiḥ//
SSu.5.8.76/ saviṣaṃ nirviṣaṃ caitadityevaṃ pariśaṅkite/

SSu.5.8.77/ agadānāṃ hi saṃyogo viṣajuṣṭasya yujyate/
SL1002/ nirviṣe mānave yukto+agadaḥ saṃpadyate+asukham//

SL1004/ ajñātvā viṣasadbhāvaṃ bhiṣagvyāpādayennaram//
SSu.5.8.79/ prodbhidyamānastu yathā+aṅkureṇa na vyaktajātiḥ pravibhāti vṛkṣaḥ/
SL1006/ tadvaddurālakṣyatamaṃ hi tāsāṃ viṣaṃ śarīre pravikīrṇamātram//

SSu.5.8.81/ tryaheṇa taddarśayatīha rūpaṃ viṣaṃ caturthe+ahani kopameti/

SSu.5.8.87/ daṃśaṃ tu mūtreṇa sakṛṣṇamadhyaṃ saraktaparyanmavehi dīrṇam/

SL1028/ vaśiṣṭhaṃ kopayāmāsa gatvā++āśramapadaṃ kila//

SL1030/ apatan darśanādeva †svestatsamatejasaḥ//

SL1033/ apakārāya vartante nṛpasādhanavāhane//

SSu.5.8.95/ trimaṇḍalā tathā śvetā kapilā pītikā tathā/

SSu.5.8.102/ tatrārkamūlaṃ rajanī nākulī pṛśniparṇikā/
SL1053/ pānakarmaṇi śasyante nasyālepāñjaneṣu ca//

SL1083/ mahāsugandhisāhitaiḥ pratyākhyāyāgadaḥ smṛtaḥ//
SSu.5.8.118/ daṃśe dāho+agnivaktrāyāḥ srāvo+atyarthaṃ jvarastathā/
SL1085/ †coṣakaṇḍūromaharṣā dāhavisphoṭasaṃyutaḥ//

SSu.5.8.138/ viṣebhyaḥ khalu sarvebhyaḥ karṇikāmarujāṃ sthirām/

SL1133/ cikitsitāt puṇyatamaṃ na kiṃcidapi śuśrumaḥ//
SSu.5.8.143/ ṛṣerindraprabhāvasyāmṛtayonerbhiṣagguroḥ/
SL1135/ dhārayitvā tu vimalaṃ mataṃ paramasaṃmatam/
SL1136/ uktāhārasamācāra iha pretya ca modate//

<B 6>

uttaratantram
<B 1>

prathamo+adhyāyaḥ/
SSu.6.1.3/ adhyāyānāṃ śate viṃśe yaduktamasakṛnmayā/
SL1138/ vakṣyāmi bahudhā samyaguttare+arthānimāniti//
SSu.6.1.4/ idānīṃ tat pravakṣyāmi tantramuttaramuttamam/
SL1140/ nikhilenopadiśyante yatra rogāḥ pṛthagvidhāḥ//
SSu.6.1.5/ śālākyatantrābhihitā videhādhipakīrtitāḥ/
SL1142/ ye ca vistarato dṛṣṭāḥ kumārābādhahetavaḥ//
SSu.6.1.6/ ṣaṭsu kāyacikitsāsu ye coktāḥ paramarṣibhiḥ/
SL1144/ upasargādayo rogā ye cāpyāgantavaḥ smṛtāḥ//
SSu.6.1.7/ triṣaṣṭī rasasaṃsargāḥ svasthavṛttaṃ tathaiva ca/
SL1146/ yuktārthā yuktayaścaiva doṣabhedāstathaiva ca//
SSu.6.1.8/ yatroktā vividhā arthā rogasādhanahetavaḥ/
SL1148/ mahatastasya tantrasya durgādhasyāmbudheriva//
SSu.6.1.9/ ādāvevottamāṅgasthān rogānabhidadhāmyaham/
SL1150/ saṅkhyayā lakṣaṇaiścāpi sādhyāsādhyakrameṇa ca//
SSu.6.1.10/ vidyāddvayaṅgulabāhulyaṃ svāṅguṣṭhodarasaṃmitam/
SL1152/ dvyaṅgulaṃ sarvataḥ sārdhaṃ bhiṣaṅnayanabudbudam//
SSu.6.1.11/ suvṛttaṃ gostanākāraṃ sarvabhūtaguṇodbhavam/
SL1154/ palaṃ bhuvo+agnito raktaṃ vātāt kṛṣṇaṃ sitaṃ jalāt//
SSu.6.1.12/ ākāśādaśrumārgāśca jāyante netrabudbude/
SL1156/ dṛṣṭiṃ cātra tathā vakṣye yathā brūyādviśāradaḥ//
SSu.6.1.13/ netrāyāmatribhāgaṃ tu kṛṣṇamaṇḍalamucyate/
SL1158/ kṛṣṇāt saptamamicchanti dṛṣṭiṃ dṛṣṭiviśāradāḥ//
SSu.6.1.14/ maṇḍalāni ca sandhīṃśca paṭalāni ca locane/
SL1160/ yathākramaṃ vijānīyāt pañca ṣaṭ ca ṣaḍeva ca//
SSu.6.1.15/ pakṣmavartmaśvetakṛṣṇadṛṣṭīnāṃ maṇḍalāni tu/
SL1162/ anupūrvaṃ tu te madhyāścatvāro+antyā yathottaram//
SSu.6.1.16/ pakṣmavartmagataḥ sandhirvartmaśuklagato+aparaḥ/
SL1164/ śuklakṛṣṇagatastvanyaḥ kṛṣṇadṛṣṭigato+aparaḥ/
SL1165/ tataḥ kanīnakagataḥ ṣaṣṭhaścāpāṅgagaḥ smṛtaḥ//
SSu.6.1.17/ dve vartmapaṭale vidyāccatvāryanyāni cākṣiṇi/
SL1167/ jāyate timiraṃ yeṣu vyādhiḥ paramadāruṇaḥ//
SSu.6.1.18/ tejojalāśritaṃ bāhyaṃ teṣvanyat piśitāśritam/
SL1169/ medastṛtīyaṃ paṭalamāśritaṃ tvasthi cāparam//
SSu.6.1.19/ pañcamāṃśasamaṃ dṛṣṭesteṣāṃ bāhulpamiṣyate/
SL1171/ sirāṇāṃ kaṇḍarāṇāṃ ca medasaḥ kālakasya ca//
SSu.6.1.20/ guṇāḥ kālāt paraḥ śleṣmā bandhane+akṣṇoḥ sirāyutaḥ/
SL1173/ sirānusāribhirdoṣairviguṇairūrdhvamāgataiḥ//
SSu.6.1.21/ jāyante netrabhāgeṣu rogāḥ paramadāruṇāḥ/
SL1175/ tatrāvilaṃ sasaṃrambhamaśrukaṇḍūpadehavat†//
SSu.6.1.22/ gurūṣātodarāgādyairjuṣṭaṃ cāvyaktalakṣaṇaiḥ/
SL1177/ saśūlaṃ vartmakoṣeṣu śūkapūrṇābhameva ca//
SSu.6.1.23/ vihanyamānaṃ rūpe vā kriyāsvakṣi yathā purā/
SL1179/ dṛṣṭvaiva dhīmān budhyeta doṣeṇādhiṣṭhitaṃ tu tat//
SSu.6.1.24/ tatra saṃbhavamāsādya yathādoṣaṃ bhiṣagjitam/
SL1181/ vidadhyānnetrajā rogā balavantaḥ syuranyathā//
SSu.6.1.25/ saṅkṣepataḥ kriyāyogo nidānaparivarjanam/
SL1183/ vātādīnāṃ pratīghātaḥ prokto vistarataḥ punaḥ//
SSu.6.1.26/ uṣṇābhitaptasya jalapraveśāddūrekṣaṇāt svapnaviparyayācca/
SL1185/ prasaktasaṃrodanakopaśokakleśābhighātādatimaithunācca//
SSu.6.1.27/ śuktāranālāmlakulatthamāṣaniṣevaṇādvegavinigrahācca/
SL1187/ svedādatho dhūmaniṣevaṇācca chardervighātādvamanātiyogāt/
SL1188/ bāṣpagrahāt sūkṣmanirīkṣaṇācca netre vikārān janayanti doṣāḥ//
SSu.6.1.28/ vātāddaśa tathā pittāt kaphāccaiva trayodaśa/
SL1190/ raktāt ṣoḍaśa vijñeyāḥ sarvajāḥ pañcaviṃśatiḥ//
SSu.6.1.29/ tathā bāhyau punardvau ca rogāḥ ṣaṭsaptatiḥ smṛtāḥ/
SL1192/ hatādhimantho nimiṣo dṛṣṭirgambhīrikā ca yā//
SSu.6.1.30/ yacca vātahataṃ vartma na te sidhyanti vātajāḥ/
SL1194/ yāpyo+atha tanmayaḥ kācaḥ sādhyāḥ syuḥsānyamārutāḥ//
SSu.6.1.31/ śuṣkākṣipākādhīmanthasyandamārutapryayāḥ/
SL1196/ asādhyo hrasvajāḍyo yo jalasrāvaśca paittikaḥ//
SSu.6.1.32/ parimlāyī ca nīlaśca yāpyaḥ kāco+atha tanmayaḥ/
SL1198/ abhiṣyando+adhimantho+amlādhyuṣitaṃ śuktikā ca yā//
SSu.6.1.33/ dṛṣṭiḥ pittavidagdhā ca dhūmadarśī ca sidhyati/
SL1200/ asādhyaḥ kaphajaḥ srāvo yāpyaḥ kācaśca tanmayaḥ//
SSu.6.1.34/ abhiṣyando+adhimanthaśca balāsagrathitaṃ ca yat/
SL1202/ dṛṣṭiḥ śleṣmavidagdhā ca pothakyo lagaṇaśca yaḥ//
SSu.6.1.35/ †krimigranthipariklinnavartmaśuklārmapiṣṭakāḥ/
SL1204/ śleṣmopanāhaḥ sādhyāstu kathitāḥ śleṣmajeṣu tu//
SSu.6.1.36/ raktasrāvo+ajakājātaṃ śoṇitārśovraṇānvitam†/
SL1206/ śukraṃ na sādhyaṃ kācaśca yāpyastajjaḥ prakīrtitaḥ//
SSu.6.1.37/ manthasyandau †kliṣṭavartma harṣotpātau tathaiva ca/
SL1208/ sirājātā+añjanākhyā ca sirājālaṃ ca yat smṛtam//
SSu.6.1.38/ parvaṇyathāvraṇaṃ śukraṃ śoṇitārmārjunaśca yaḥ/
SL1210/ ete sādhyā vikāreṣu raktajeṣu bhavanti hi//
SSu.6.1.39/ pūyāsrāvo nākulāndhyamakṣipākātyayo+alajī/
SL1212/ asādhyāḥ sarvajā yāpyaḥ kācaḥ kopaśca pakṣmaṇaḥ//
SSu.6.1.40/ vartmāvabandho yo vyādhiḥ sirāsu piḍakā ca yā/
SL1214/ prastāryarmādhimāṃsārma snāyvarmotsaṅginī ca yā//
SSu.6.1.41/ pūyālasaścārbudaṃ ca śyāvakardamavartmanī/
SL1216/ tathā+arśovartma śuṣkārśaḥ śarkarāvartma yacca vai//
SSu.6.1.42/ saśophaścāpyaśophaśca pāko bahalavartma ca/
SL1218/ aklinnavartma kumbhīkā bisavartma ca sidhyati//
SSu.6.1.43/ sanimitto+animittaśca dvāvasādhyau tu bāhyajau/
SL1220/ ṣaṭsaptatirvikārāṇāmeṣā saṃgrahakīrtitā†//
SSu.6.1.44/ nava sandhyāśrayāsteṣu vartmajāstvekaviṃśatiḥ/
SL1222/ śuklabhāge daśaikaśca catvāraḥ kṛṣṇabhāgajāḥ//
SSu.6.1.45/ sarvāśrayāḥ saptadaśa dṛṣṭijā dvādaśaiva tu/
SL1224/ bāhyajau dvau samākhyātau rogau paramadāruṇau/
SL1225/ bhūya etān pravakṣyāmi saṅkhyārūpacikitsitaiḥ//

<B 2>

dvitīyo+adhyāyaḥ/
SSu.6.2.3/ pūyālasaḥ sopanāhaḥ srāvāḥ parvaṇikā+alajī/
SL1227/ krimigranthiśca vijñeyā rogāḥ sandhigatā nava//

SL1231/ tān vai srāvān netranāḍīmathaike tasyā liṅgaṃ kīrtayiṣye caturdhā//

SL1233/ śvetaṃ sāndraṃ picchilaṃ saṃsravedyaḥ śleṣmāsrāvo nīrujaḥ sa pradiṣṭaḥ//

SL1237/ jātā sandhau kṛṣṇaśukle+alajī syāttasminneva khyāpitā pūrvaliṅgaiḥ//
SSu.6.2.9/ krimigranthirvartmanaḥ pakṣmaṇaśca kaṇḍūṃ kuryuḥ krimayaḥ sandhijātāḥ/

<B 3>

tṛtīyo+adhyāyaḥ/
SSu.6.3.3/ pṛthagdoṣāḥ samastā vā yadā vartmavyapāśrayāḥ/
SL1241/ sirā vyāpyāvatiṣṭhante vartmasvadhikamūrcchitāḥ//
SSu.6.3.4/ †vivardhya māṃsaṃ raktaṃ ca tadā vartmavyapāśrayān/
SL1243/ vikārāñjanayantyāśu nāmatastānnibodhata//
SSu.6.3.5/ utsaṅginyatha kumbhīkā pothakyo vartmaśarkarā/
SL1245/ tathā+arśovartma śuṣkārśastathaivāñjananāmikā//
SSu.6.3.6/ bahalaṃ vartma yaccāpi vyādhirvartmāvabandhakaḥ/
SL1247/ kliṣṭakardamavartmākhyau śyāvavartma tathaiva ca//
SSu.6.3.7/ praklinnamapariklinnaṃ vartma vātahataṃ tu yat/
SL1249/ arbudaṃ nimiṣaścāpi śoṇitārśaśca yat smṛtam//
SSu.6.3.8/ lagaṇo biśanāmā ca pakṣmakopastathaiva ca/
SL1251/ ekaviṃśatirityete vikārā vartmasaṃśrayāḥ//
SSu.6.3.9/ nāmabhiste samuddiṣṭā lakṣaṇaistān pracakṣmahe/
SL1253/ abhyantaramukhī bāhyotsaṅge+adho vartmanaśca† yā//
SSu.6.3.10/ vijñeyotsaṅginī nāma tadrūpapiḍakācitā/
SL1255/ kumbhīkabījapratimāḥ piḍakā †yāstu vartmajāḥ//
SSu.6.3.11/ †ādhmāpayanti bhinnā yāḥ kumbhīkapiḍakāstu tāḥ/
SL1257/ srāviṇyaḥ †kaṇḍurā gurvyo raktasarṣapasannibhāḥ/
SL1258/ piḍakāśca rujāvatyaḥ pothakya iti saṃjñitāḥ//
SSu.6.3.12/ piḍakābhiḥ susūkṣmābhirghanābhirabhisaṃvṛtā/
SL1260/ piḍakā yā kharā sthūlā sā jñeyā vartmaśarkarā//
SSu.6.3.13/ †ervārubījapratimāḥ piḍakā mandavedanāḥ/
SL1262/ sūkṣmāḥ kharāśca vartmasthāstadarśovartma kīrtyate//
SSu.6.3.14/ dīrgho+aṅkuraḥ kharaḥ stabdho dāruṇo vartmasaṃbhavaḥ/
SL1264/ vyādhireṣa samākhyātaḥ śuṣkārśa iti saṃjñitaḥ//
SSu.6.3.15/ dāhatodavatī tāmrā piḍakā vartmasaṃbhavā/
SL1266/ mṛdvī mandarujā sūkṣmā jñeyā sā+añjananāmikā//
SSu.6.3.16/ vartmopacīyate yasya piḍakābhiḥ samantataḥ/
SL1268/ savarṇābhiḥ samābhiśca vidyādbahalavartma tat//
SSu.6.3.17/ kaṇḍūmatā+alpatodena vartmaśophena yo naraḥ/
SL1270/ na samaṃ chādayedakṣi bhavedbandhaḥ sa vartmanaḥ//
SSu.6.3.18/ mṛdvalpavedanaṃ tāmraṃ yadvartma samameva ca/
SL1272/ akasmācca bhavedraktaṃ kliṣṭavartma tadādiśet//
SSu.6.3.19/ kliṣṭaṃ punaḥ pittayutaṃ vidahecchoṇitaṃ yadā/
SL1274/ tadā klinnatvamāpannamucyate vartmakardamaḥ//
SSu.6.3.20/ yadvartma bāhyato+anyaśca śyāvaṃ śūnaṃ savedanam/
SL1276/ dāhakaṇḍūparikledi† śyāvavartmeti tanmatam//
SSu.6.3.21/ arujaṃ bāhyataḥ śūnamantaḥ klinnaṃ sravatyapi/
SL1278/ kaṇḍūnistodabhūyiṣṭhaṃ klinnavartma taducyate//
SSu.6.3.22/ yasya dhautāni dhautāni saṃbadhyante punaḥ punaḥ/
SL1280/ vartmānyaparipakvāni vidyādaklinnavartma tat//
SSu.6.3.23/ vimuktasandhi niśceṣṭaṃ vartma yasya na mīlyate/
SL1282/ etadvātahataṃ vidyāt sarujaṃ yadi vā+arujam//
SSu.6.3.24/ vartmāntarasthaṃ viṣamaṃ granthibhūtamavedanam/
SL1284/ vijñeyamarbudaṃ puṃsāṃ saraktamavalambitam//
SSu.6.3.25/ nimeṣaṇīḥ sirā vāyuḥ praviṣṭo vartmasaṃśrayāḥ/
SL1286/ †cālayatyati vartmāni nimeṣaḥ sa gado mataḥ//
SSu.6.3.26/ chinnāśchinā vivardhante vartmasthā mṛdavo+aṅkurāḥ/
SL1288/ dāhakaṇḍūrujopetāste+arśaḥ śoṇitasaṃbhavāḥ†//
SSu.6.3.27/ apākaḥ kaṭhinaḥ sthūlo granthirvartmabhavo+arujaḥ/
SL1290/ sakaṇḍūḥ picchilaḥ kolapramāṇo lagaṇastu saḥ//
SSu.6.3.28/ śūnaṃ yadvartma bahubhiḥ sūkṣmaiśchidraiḥ samanvitam/
SL1292/ bisamantarjala iva bisavartmeti tanmatam//
SSu.6.3.29/ doṣāḥ pakṣmāśayagatāstīkṣṇāgrāṇi kharāṇi ca/
SL1294/ nirvartayanti pakṣmāṇi tairghuṣṭaṃ cākṣi dūyate//
SSu.6.3.30/ uddhṛtairuddhṛtaiḥ† śāntiḥ pakṣmabhiścopajāyate/
SL1296/ vātātapānaladveṣī pakṣmakopaḥ sa ucyate//

caturtho+adhyāyaḥ/
SSu.6.4.3/ prastāriśuklakṣatajādhimāṃsasrāyvarmasaṃjñāḥ khalu pañca rogāḥ/
SL1298/ syuḥ śuktikā cārjunapiṣṭakau ca jālaṃ sirāṇāṃ piḍakāśca yāḥ syuḥ//

SL1300/ prastāri prathitamihārma śuklabhāge vistīrṇaṃ tanu rudhiraprabhaṃ sanīlam//
SSu.6.4.5/ śuklākhyaṃ mṛdu kathayanti śuklabhāge saśvetaṃ samamiha† vardhate cireṇa/
SL1302/ yanmāṃsaṃ pracayamupaiti śuklabhāge padmābhaṃ tadupadiśanti lohitārma//
SSu.6.4.6/ vistīrṇaṃ mṛdu bahalaṃ yakṛtprakāśaṃ śyāvaṃ vā tadadhikamāṃsajārma vidyāt/
SL1304/ śukle yat piśitamupaiti vṛddhimetat snāyvarmetyabhipaṭhitaṃ kharaṃ prapāṇḍu//
SSu.6.4.7/ śyāvāḥ syuḥ piśitanibhāśca bindavo ye śuktyābhāḥ sitanayane sa śuktisaṃjñaḥ/
SL1306/ eko yaḥ śaśarudhiropamastu binduḥ śuklastho bhavati tamarjunaṃ vadanti//
SSu.6.4.8/ utsannaḥ salilanibho+atha piṣṭaśuklo binduryo bhavati sa piṣṭakaḥ suvṛttaḥ/
SL1308/ jālābhaḥ kaṭhinasiro mahān saraktaḥ santānaḥ smṛta iha jālasaṃjñitastu//
SSu.6.4.9/ śuklasthāḥ sitapiḍakāḥ sirāvṛtā yāstā vidyādasitasamīpajāḥ sirājāḥ/
SL1310/ kāṃsyābho† bhavati site+ambubindutulyaḥ sa jñeyo+amṛdurarujo balāsakākhyaḥ//

<B 5>

pañcamo+adhyāyaḥ/
SSu.6.5.3/ yat savraṇaṃ śukla(śukra)mathāvraṇaṃ vā pākātyayaścāpyajakā tathaiva/
SL1312/ catvāra ete+abhihitā vikārāḥ kṛṣṇāśrayāḥ saṃgrahataḥ purastāt//
SSu.6.5.4/ nimagnarūpaṃ hi bhavettu kṛṣṇe sūcyeva viddhaṃ pratibhāti †yadvai/
SL1314/ srāvaṃ sraveduṣṇamatīva ruk ca tat savraṇaṃ śukra(śukla)mudāharanti//
SSu.6.5.5/ dṛṣṭeḥ samīpe na bhavettu yacca na cāvagāḍhaṃ na ca saṃsraveddhi/
SL1316/ avedanāvanna ca yugmaśukraṃ tatsiddhimāpnoti kadācideva//
SSu.6.5.6/ vicchinnamadhyaṃ piśitāvṛtaṃ vā calaṃ sirāsaktamadṛṣṭikṛcca†/
SL1318/ dvitvaggataṃ lohitamantataśca cirotthitaṃ cāpi vivarjanīyam//
SSu.6.5.7/ uṣṇāśrupātaḥ piḍakā ca kṛṣṇe yasmin bhavenmudganibhaṃ ca śukram/
SL1320/ tadapyasādhyaṃ pravadanti kecidanyacca yattittiripakṣatulyam//
SSu.6.5.8/ sitaṃ yadā bhātyasitapradeśe syandātmakaṃ nātirugaśruyuktam/
SL1322/ vihāyasīvācchaghanānukāri† tadavraṇaṃ sādhyatamaṃ vadanti//
SSu.6.5.9/ gambhīrajātaṃ bahalaṃ ca śukraṃ cirotthitaṃ cāpi vadanti kṛcchram/
SL1324/ saṃcchādyate† śvetanibhena sarvaṃ doṣeṇa yasyāsitamaṇḍalaṃ tu//
SSu.6.5.10/ tamakṣipākātyayamakṣikopasamutthitaṃ† tīvrarujaṃ vadanti/
SL1326/ ajāpurīṣapratimo rujāvān salohito lohitapicchilāśruḥ/
SL1327/ vidārya kṛṣṇaṃ pracayo+abhyupaiti taṃ cājakājātamiti vyavasyet//

<B 6>

ṣaṣṭho+adhyāyaḥ/
SSu.6.6.3/ syandāstu catvāra ihopadiṣṭāstāvanta eveha tathā+adhimanthāḥ/
SL1329/ śophānvito+aśophayutaśca pākāvityevamete daśa saṃpradiṣṭāḥ//
SSu.6.6.4/ hatādhimantho+anilaparyayaśca śuṣkākṣipāko+anyata eva vātaḥ/
SL1331/ dṛṣṭistatathā+amlādhyuṣitā sirāṇāmutpātaharṣāvapi sarvabhāgāḥ//
SSu.6.6.5/ prāyeṇa sarve nayanāmayāstu bhavantyabhiṣyandanimittamūlāḥ/
SL1333/ tasmādabhiṣyandamudīryamāṇamupācaredāśu hitāya dhīmān//
SSu.6.6.6/ nistodanaṃ stambhanaromaharṣasaṅgharṣapāruṣyaśirobhitāpāḥ/
SL1335/ viśuṣkabhāvaḥ śiśirāśrutā ca vātābhipanne nayane bhavanti//
SSu.6.6.7/ dāhaprapākau śiśirābhinandā dhūmāyanaṃ bāṣpasamucchrayaśca/
SL1337/ uṣṇāśrutā pītakanetratā ca pittābhipanne nayane bhavanti//
SSu.6.6.8/ uṣṇābhinandā gurutā+akṣiśophaḥ kaṇḍūpadehau sitatā+atiśaityam/
SL1339/ srāvo muhuḥ picchila eva cāpi kaphābhipanne nayane bhavanti//
SSu.6.6.9/ tāmrāśrutā lohitanetratā ca rājyaḥ samantādatilohitāśca/
SL1341/ pittasya liṅgāni ca yāni tāni raktābhipanne nayane bhavanti//
SSu.6.6.10/ vṛddhairetairabhiṣyandairnarāṇāmakriyāvatām/
SL1343/ tāvantastvadhimanthāḥ syurnayane tīvravedanāḥ//
SSu.6.6.11/ utpāṭyata ivātyarthaṃ netraṃ nirmathyate tathā/
SL1345/ śiraso+ardhaṃ ca taṃ vidyādadhimanthaṃ svalakṣaṇaiḥ//
SSu.6.6.12/ netramutpāṭyata iva mathyate+araṇivacca yat/
SL1347/ saṅgharṣatodanirbhedamāṃsasaṃrabdhamāvilam//
SSu.6.6.13/ kuñcanāsphoṭanādhmānavepathuvyathanairyutam/
SL1349/ śiraso+ardhaṃ ca yena syādadhimanthaḥ sa mārutāt//
SSu.6.6.14/ raktarājicitaṃ srāvi vahninevāvadahyate/
SL1351/ yakṛtpiṇḍopamaṃ dāhi kṣāreṇāktamiva kṣatam//
SSu.6.6.15/ prapakvocchūnavartmāntaṃ sasvedaṃ pītadarśanam/
SL1353/ mūrcchāśirodāhayutaṃ pittenākṣyadhimanthitam//
SSu.6.6.16/ śophavannātisaṃrabdhaṃ srāvakaṇḍūsamanvitam/
SL1355/ śaityagauravapaicchilyadūṣikāharṣaṇānvitam//
SSu.6.6.17/ rūpaṃ paśyati duḥkhena pāṃśupūrṇamivāvilam/
SL1357/ nāsādhmānaśiroduḥkhayutaṃ śleṣmādhimanthitam//
SSu.6.6.18/ bandhujīvapratīkāśaṃ tāmyati sparśanākṣamam/
SL1359/ raktāsrāvaṃ sanistodaṃ paśyatyagninibhā diśaḥ//
SSu.6.6.19/ raktāmagnāriṣṭavacca kṛṣṇabhāgaśca lakṣyate/
SL1361/ yaddīptaṃ raktaparyantaṃ tadraktenādhimanthitam//
SSu.6.6.20/ hanyādṛṣṭiṃ saptarātrāt kaphottho+adhīmantho+asṛksaṃbhavaḥ pañcarātrāt/
SL1363/ ṣaḍrātrādvau mārutottho nihanyānmithyācārāt paittikaḥ sadya eva//
SSu.6.6.21/ kaṇḍūpadehāśruyutaḥ pakvodumbarasannibhaḥ/
SL1365/ dāhasaṃharṣatāmratvaśophanistodagauravaiḥ//
SSu.6.6.22/ juṣṭo muhuḥ sraveccāsramuṣṇaśītāmbu picchilam/
SL1367/ saṃrambhī pacyate yaśca netrapākaḥ saśophajaḥ//
SSu.6.6.23/ śophahīnāni liṅgāni netrapāke tvaśophaje/
SL1369/ upekṣaṇādakṣi yadā+adhimantho vātātmakaḥ sādayati prasahya/
SL1370/ rujābhirugrābhirasādhya eṣa hatādhimanthaḥ khalu nāma rogaḥ//
SSu.6.6.24/ antaḥsirāṇāṃ śvasanaḥ sthito dṛṣṭiṃ pratikṣipan/
SL1372/ hatādhimanthaṃ janayettamasādhyaṃ vidurbudhāḥ//
SSu.6.6.25/ pakṣmadvayākṣibhruvamāśritastu yannānilaḥ saṃcarati praduṣṭaḥ/
SL1374/ paryāyaśaścāpi rujaḥ karoti taṃ vātaparyāyamudāharanti//
SSu.6.6.26/ yat kūṇitaṃ dāruṇarūkṣavartma vilokane cāviladarśanaṃ yat/
SL1376/ sudāruṇaṃ yat pratibodhane ca śuṣkākṣipākopahataṃ tadakṣi//
SSu.6.6.27/ yasyāvaṭūkarṇaśirohanustho manyāgato vā+apyanilo+anyato vā/
SL1378/ kuryādrujo+ati bhruvi locane vā tamanyatovātamudāharanti//
SSu.6.6.28/ amlena bhuktena vidāhinā ca saṃchādyate sarvata eva netram/
SL1380/ śophānvitaṃ lohitakaiḥ sanīlairetādṛgamlādhyuṣitaṃ vadanti//
SSu.6.6.29/ avedanā vā+api savedanā vā yasyākṣirājyo hi bhavanti tāmrāḥ/
SL1382/ muhurvirajyanti ca tāḥ samantād vyādhiḥ sirotpāta iti pradiṣṭaḥ//
SSu.6.6.30/ mohāt sirotpāta upekṣitastu jāyeta rogastu sirāpraharṣaḥ/
SL1384/ tāmrācchamasraṃ† sravati pragāḍhaṃ tathā na śaknotyabhivīkṣituṃ ca//

<B 7>

saptamo+adhyāyaḥ/
SSu.6.7.3/ marūradalamātrāṃ tu pañcabhūtaprasādajām/

SL1402/ mahāntyapi ca rūpāṇi cchāditānīva vāsasā//

SL1410/ timirākhyaḥ sa vai doṣaḥ caturthaṃ paṭalaṃ gataḥ//

SL1412/ tasminnapi tamobhūte nātirūḍhe mahāgade//
SSu.6.7.17/ candrādityau sanakṣatrāvantarīkṣe ca vidyutaḥ/
SL1414/ nirmalāni ca tejāṃsi bhrājiṣṇūni† ca paśyati//
SSu.6.7.18/ sa eva liṅganāśastu nīlikākācasaṃjñitaḥ/
SL1416/ tatra vātena rūpāṇi bhramantīva sa paśyati//

SL1418/ pittenādityakhadyotaśakracāpataḍidguṇān//

SL1422/ paśyedasūkṣmāṇyatyarthaṃ vyabhre caivābhrasaṃplavam//

SL1425/ sannipātena citrāṇi viplutāni ca paśyati//
SSu.6.7.24/ bahudhā vā dvidhā vā+api sarvāṇyeva samantataḥ/

SL1429/ pītā diśastathodyantamādityamiva paśyati//

SL1447/ tathā naraḥ pittavidagdhadṛṣṭiḥ kaphena cānyastvatha dhūmadarśī//

<B 8>

aṣṭamo+adhyāyaḥ/
SSu.6.8.3/ ṣaṭsaptatirye+abhihitā vyādhayo nāmalakṣaṇaiḥ/
SL1472/ cikitsitamidaṃ teṣāṃ samāsavyāsataḥ† śṛṇu//
SSu.6.8.4/ chedyāsteṣu daśaikaśca nava lekhyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ/
SL1474/ bhedyāḥ pañca vikārāḥ syurvyadhyāḥ pañcadaśaiva tu//
SSu.6.8.5/ dvādaśā+aśastrakṛtyāśca yāpyāḥ sapta bhavanti hi/
SL1476/ rogā varjayitavyāḥ syurdaśa pañca ca jānatā/
SL1477/ asādhyau vā bhavetāṃ tu yāpyau cāgantusaṃjñitau//
SSu.6.8.6/ arśo+anvitaṃ bhavati vartma tu yattathā+arśaḥ śuṣkaṃ tathā+arbudamatho piḍakāḥ sirājāḥ/
SL1479/ jālaṃ sirājamapi pañcavidhaṃ tathā+arma chedyā bhavanti saha parvaṇikāmayena//
SSu.6.8.7/ utsaṅginī bahalakardamavartmanī ca śyāvaṃ ca yacca paṭhitaṃ tviha baddhavartma/
SL1481/ kliṣṭaṃ ca pothakiyutaṃ khalu yacca vartma kumbhīkinī ca saha śarkarayā ca lekhyāḥ//
SSu.6.8.8/ śleṣmopanāhalagaṇau ca bisaṃ ca bhedyā granthiśca yaḥ kṛmikṛto+añjananāmikā ca/
SL1483/ ādau sirā nigaditāstu yayoḥ prayoge pākau ca yau nayanayoḥ pavano+anyataśca//
SSu.6.8.9/ pūyālasānilaviparyayamanthasaṃjñāḥ syandāstu yantyupaśamaṃ hi sirāvyadhena/
SL1485/ śuṣkākṣipākakaphapittavidagdhadṛṣṭiṣvamlākhyaśukrasahitārjunapiṣṭakeṣu//
SSu.6.8.10/ aklinnavartmahutabhugdhvajadarśiśuktipraklinnavartmasu tathaiva balāsasaṃjñe/
SL1487/ āgantunā++āmayayugena ca dūṣitāyāṃ dṛṣṭau na śastrapatanaṃ pravadanti tajjñāḥ//
SSu.6.8.11/ saṃpaśyataḥ ṣaḍapi ye+abhihitāstu kācāste pakṣmakopasahitāstu bhavanti yāpyāḥ/
SL1489/ catvāra eva pavanaprabhavāstvasādhyā dvau pittajau kaphanimittaja eka eva/
SL1490/ aṣṭārdhakā rudhirajāśca gadāstridoṣāstāvanta eva gaditāvapi bāhyajau dvau//

SL1520/ anenaiva vidhānena bhiṣak tāvapi sādhayet//

SL1522/ vṛkṣādanyāṃ kapitthe ca pañcamūle mahatyapi//

<B 10>

daśamo+adhyāyaḥ/
SSu.6.10.3/ pittasyande paittike cādhimanthe raktāsrāvaḥ sraṃsanaṃ cāpi kāryam/
SL1538/ akṣṇoḥ sekālepanasyāñjanāni paitte ca syādyadvisarpe vidhānam//
SSu.6.10.4/ gundrāṃ śāliṃ śaivalaṃ śailabhedaṃ dārvīmelāmutpalaṃ rodhramabhram/
SL1540/ padmātpatraṃ śarkarā darbhamikṣuṃ tālaṃ rodhraṃ vetasaṃ padmakaṃ ca//
SSu.6.10.5/ drākṣāṃ kṣaudraṃ candanaṃ yaṣṭikāhvaṃ yoṣitkṣīraṃ rātryanante ca piṣṭvā/
SL1542/ sarpiḥsiddhaṃ tarpaṇe sekanasye† śastaṃ kṣīraṃ siddhameteṣu cājam//
SSu.6.10.6/ yojyo vargo vyasta eṣo+anyathā vā samyaṅnasye+aṣṭārdhasaṃkhye+api nityam/
SL1544/ kriyāḥ sarvāḥ pittaharyaḥ praśastāstryahāccordhvaṃ kṣīrasarpiśca †nasyam//
SSu.6.10.7/ pālāśaṃ syācchoṇitaṃ cāñjanārthe śallakyā vā śarkarākṣaudrayuktam/
SL1546/ rasakriyāṃ śarkarākṣaudrayuktāṃ pālindyāṃ vā madhuke vā+api kuryāt//
SSu.6.10.8/ mustā phenaḥ sāgarasyotpalaṃ ca kṛmighnailādhātribījādrasaśca/
SL1548/ tālīśailāgairikośīraśaṅkhairevaṃ yuñjyādañjanaṃ stanyapiṣṭaiḥ//
SSu.6.10.9/ cūrṇaṃ kuryādañjanārthe raso vā stanyopeto dhātakīsyandanābhyām/
SL1550/ yoṣitstanyaṃ śātakumbhaṃ vighṛṣṭaṃ kṣaudropetaṃ kaiśukaṃ cāpi puṣpam//
SSu.6.10.10/ rodhraṃ drākṣāṃ śarkarāmutpalaṃ ca nāryāḥ kṣīre yaṣṭikāhvaṃ vacāṃ ca/
SL1552/ piṣṭvā kṣīre varṇakasya tvacaṃ ca toyonmiśre candanodumbare ca//
SSu.6.10.11/ kāryaḥ phenaḥ sāgarasyāñjanārthe nārīstanye mākṣike cāpi ghṛṣṭaḥ/
SL1554/ yoṣitstanye sthāpitaṃ yaṣṭikāhvaṃ rodhraṃ drākṣāṃ śarkarāmutpalaṃ ca//
SSu.6.10.12/ kṣaumābaddhaṃ pathyamāścyotane vā †sarpirghṛṣṭaṃ yaṣṭikāhvaṃ sarodhram/
SL1556/ toyonmiśrāḥ kāśmarīdhātripathyāstadvaccāhuḥ kaṭphalaṃ cāmbunaiva//
SSu.6.10.13/ eṣo+amlākhye+anukramaścāpi śuktau kāryaḥ sarvaḥ syātsirāmokṣavarjyaḥ//
SSu.6.10.14/ sarpiḥ peyaṃ traiphalaṃ tailvakaṃ vā peyaṃ vā syāt kevalaṃ yat purāṇam/
SL1559/ doṣe+adhastācchuktikāyāmapāste śītairdravyairañjanaṃ kāryamāśu//
SSu.6.10.15/ vaidūryaṃ yat sphāṭikaṃ vaidrumaṃ ca mauktaṃ śāṅkhaṃ rājataṃ śātakumbham/
SL1561/ cūrṇaṃ sūkṣmaṃ śarkarākṣaudrayuktaṃ śuktiṃ hanyādañjanaṃ caitadāśu//
SSu.6.10.16/ yuñjyāt sarpirdhūmadarśī narastu śeṣaṃ kuryādraktapitte vidhānam/
SL1563/ yaccaivānyat pittahṛccāpi sarvaṃ yadvīsarpe paittike vai vidhānam//

<B 11>

ekādaśo+adhyāyaḥ/
SSu.6.11.3/ syandādhimanthau kaphajau pravṛddhau jayet sirāṇāmatha mokṣaṇena/
SL1565/ svedāvapīḍāñjanadhūmasekapralepayogaiḥ kavalagrahaiśca//
SSu.6.11.4/ rūkṣaistathā++āścyotanasaṃvidhānaistathaiva rūksaiḥ puṭapākayogaiḥ/
SL1567/ tryahāttryahāccāpyapatarpaṇānte prātastayostiktaghṛtaṃ praśastam//
SSu.6.11.5/ tadannapānaṃ ca samācareddhi yacchleṣmaṇo naiva karoti vṛddhim/
SL1569/ kuṭannaṭāsphoṭaphaṇijjñabilvapattūrapilvarkakapitthabhaṅgaiḥ//
SSu.6.11.6/ svedaṃ vidadhyāt athavā+anulepaṃ barhiṣṭhaśuṇṭhīsurakāṣṭhakuṣṭhaiḥ/
SL1571/ sindhūtthahiṅgutriphalāmadhūkaprapauṇḍarīkāñjanatutthatāmraiḥ//
SSu.6.11.7/ piṣṭairjalenāñjanavartayaḥ syuḥ pathyāharidrāmadhukāñjanairvā/
SL1573/ trīṇyūṣaṇāni triphalā haridrā viḍaṅgasāraśca samāni ca syuḥ//
SSu.6.11.8/ barhiṣṭhakuṣṭhāmarakāṣṭhaśaṅkhapāṭhāmalavyoṣamanaḥśilāśca†/
SL1575/ piṣṭvā+ambunā vā kusumāni jātikarañjaśobhāñjanajāni yuñjyāt//
SSu.6.11.9/ phalaṃ prakīryādathavā+api śigroḥ puṣpaṃ ca †tulyaṃ bṛhatīdvayasya/
SL1577/ rasāñjanaṃ saindhavacandanaṃ ca manaḥśilāle laśunaṃ ca tulyam//
SSu.6.11.10/ piṣṭvā+añjanārthe kaphajeṣu dhīmān vartīrvidadhyānnayanāmayeṣu/
SL1579/ roge balāsagrathite+añjanaṃ jñaiḥ kartavyametat suviśuddhakāye//
SSu.6.11.11/ nīlān yavān gavyapayo+anupītān śalākinaḥ śuṣkatanūn vidahya/
SL1581/ tathā+arjakāsphotakapitthabilvanirguṇḍijātīkusumāni caiva//
SSu.6.11.12/ tatkṣāravatsaindhavatuttharocanaṃ pakvaṃ vidadhyādatha lohanāḍyām/
SL1583/ etadbalāsagrathite+añjanaṃ syādeṣo+anukalpastu phaṇijjñakādau//
SSu.6.11.13/ mahauṣadhaṃ māgadhikāṃ ca mustāṃ† sasaindhavaṃ yanmaricaṃ ca śuklam/
SL1585/ tanmātuluṅgasvarasena piṣṭaṃ netrāñjanaṃ piṣṭakamāśu hanyāt//
SSu.6.11.14/ †phale bṛhatyā magadhodbhavānā nidhāya kalkaṃ phalapākakāle/
SL1587/ †srotojayuktaṃ ca taduddhṛtaṃ syāttadvattu piṣṭe vidhireṣa cāpi//
SSu.6.11.15/ vārtākaśigrvindrasurāpaṭolakirātatiktāmalakīphaleṣu/
SL1589/ kāsīsasāmudrarasāñjanāni jātyāstathā kṣārakam(korakam)eva cāpi//
SSu.6.11.16/ praklinnavartmanyupadiśyate tu yogāñjanaṃ tanmadhunā+avaghṛṣṭam/
SL1591/ nādeyamagryaṃ maricaṃ ca śuklaṃ nepālajātā ca samapramāṇā//
SSu.6.11.17/ samātuluṅgadrava eṣa yogaḥ kaṇḍūṃ nihanyāt sakṛdañjanena/
SL1593/ saśṛṅgaveraṃ suradāru mustaṃ sindhuprabhūtaṃ(prasūtaṃ) mukulāni jātyāḥ//
SSu.6.11.18/ surāprapiṣṭaṃ tvidamañjanaṃ hi kaṇḍvāṃ ca śophe ca hitaṃ vadanti/
SL1595/ syandādhimanthakramamācarecca sarveṣu caiteṣu sadā+apramattaḥ// (viśeṣato nāvanameva kāryaṃ saṃsarjanaṃ cāpi yathopadiṣṭam†)

<B 12>

dvādaśo+adhyāyaḥ/

SSu.6.12.16/ madhunā śaṅkhanaipālītutthadārvyaḥ sasaindhavāḥ/
SL1624/ rasaḥ śirīṣapuṣpācca surāmaricamākṣikaiḥ//
SSu.6.12.17/ yuktaṃ tu madhunā vā+api gairikaṃ hitamañjane/
SL1626/ sirāharṣe+añjanaṃ kuryāt phaṇitaṃ madhusaṃyutam//

SL1628/ vetrāmlastanyasaṃyuktaṃ phāṇitaṃ ca sasaindhavam//

SL1642/ kukkuṭāṇḍakapālāni laśunaṃ kaṭukatrayam//

SL1644/ puṭapākāvasānena raktavisrāvaṇādinā//

SL1648/ śirīṣabījamaricapippalīsaindhavairapi//

SL1650/ kuryāttāmrarajaḥśaṅkhaśilāmaricasaindhavaiḥ//
SSu.6.12.30/ antyāddviguṇitairebhirañjanaṃ śukranāśanam/
SL1652/ kuryādañjanayogau vā samyak ślokārdhikāvimau//

SL1654/ kṣaudradantārṇavamalaśirīṣakusumairapi//
SSu.6.12.32/ kṣārāñjanaṃ vā vitaredbalāsagrathitāpaham/
SL1656/ mudgān vā nistuṣān bhṛṣṭān śaṅkhakṣaudrasitāyutān//
SSu.6.12.33/ madhūkasāraṃ madhunā yojayeccāñjane sadā/
SL1658/ bibhītakāsthimajjā vā sakṣaudraḥ śukranāśanaḥ//

SL1660/ dvitvaggate saśūle vā vātaghnaṃ tarpaṇaṃ hitam//

SSu.6.12.39/ sekāścyotananasyāni puṭapākāṃśca kārayet/
SL1670/ sarvataścāpi śuddhasya kartavyamidamañjanam//

SL1672/ maireyaṃ vā+api dadhyevaṃ dadhyuttarakameva vā//

SL1674/ madhūkasāraṃ madhunā tulyāṃśaṃ gairikeṇa vā//

<B 13>

trayodaśo+adhyāyaḥ/
SSu.6.13.3/ nava ye+abhihitā lekhyāḥ sāmānyasteṣvayaṃ vidhiḥ/
SL1700/ snigdhavāntaviriktasya nivātātapasadmani//
SSu.6.13.4/ (āptairdṛḍhaṃ gṛhītasya veśmanyuttānaśāyinaḥ/
SL1702/ ) sukhodakaprataptena vāsasā susamāhitaḥ/
SL1703/ svedayedvartma nirbhujya vāmāṅguṣṭhāṅgulishtitam//
SSu.6.13.5/ aṅgulyaṅguṣṭhakābhyāṃ tu nirbhugnaṃ vartma yatnataḥ/
SL1705/ †plotāntarābhyāṃ na yathā calati sraṃsate+api vā//
SSu.6.13.6/ tataḥ pramṛjya plotena vartma śastrapadāṅkitam/
SL1707/ likhecchastreṇa patrairvā tato rakte sthite punaḥ//
SSu.6.13.7/ svinnaṃ manohvākāsīsavyoṣārdrāñjanasaindhavaiḥ†/
SL1709/ ślakṣṇapiṣṭaiḥ samākṣīkaiḥ pratisāryoṣṇavāriṇā//
SSu.6.13.8/ prakṣālya haviṣā siktaṃ vraṇavat samupācaret/
SL1711/ svedāvapīḍaprabhṛtīṃstryahādūrdhvaṃ prayojayet//
SSu.6.13.9/ vyāsataste samuddiṣṭaṃ vidhānaṃ lekhyakarmaṇi/
SL1713/ asṛgāsrāvarahitaṃ kaṇḍūśophavivarjitam//
SSu.6.13.10/ samaṃ nakhanibhaṃ vartma likhitaṃ samyagiṣyate/
SL1715/ raktamakṣi sravet skannaṃ kṣatācchastrakṛtāddhruvam//
SSu.6.13.11/ rāgaśophaparisrāvāstimiraṃ vyādhyanirjayaḥ/
SL1717/ vartma śyāvaṃ guru stabdhaṃ kaṇḍūharṣopadehavat//
SSu.6.13.12/ netrapākamudīrṇaṃ vā kurvītāpratikāriṇaḥ/
SL1719/ etaddurlikhitaṃ jñeyaṃ snehayitvā punarlikhet//
SSu.6.13.13/ vyāvartate yadā vartma pakṣma cāpi †vimuhyati/
SL1721/ syāt saruk srāvabahulaṃ tadatisrāvitaṃ viduḥ//
SSu.6.13.14/ snehasvedādiriṣṭaḥ syāt kramastatrānilāpahaḥ/
SL1723/ vartmāvabandhaṃ kliṣṭaṃ ca bahalaṃ yacca kīrtitam//
SSu.6.13.15/ pothakīścāpyavalikhet pracchayitvā+agrataḥ śanaiḥ/
SL1725/ samaṃ likhettu medhāvī śyāvakardamavartmanī//
SSu.6.13.16/ kumbhīkinīṃ śarkarāṃ ca tathaivotsaṅginīmapi/
SL1727/ †kalpayitvā tu śastreṇa likhet paścādatandritaḥ//
SSu.6.13.17/ bhaveyurvartmasu ca yāḥ piḍakāḥ kaṭhinā bhṛśam/
SL1729/ hrasvāstāmrāśca tāḥ pakvā bhindyādbhinnā likhedapi//
SSu.6.13.18/ taruṇīścālpasaṃrambhā piḍakā bāhyavartmajāḥ/
SL1731/ viditvaitāḥ praśamayet svedālepanaśodhanaiḥ//

<B 14>

caturdaśo+adhyāyaḥ/
SSu.6.14.3/ svedayitvā bisagranthiṃ chidrāṇyasya nirāśayam†/
SL1733/ pakvaṃ bhittvā tu śastreṇa sindhavenāvacūrṇayet//
SSu.6.14.4/ kāsīsamāgadhīpuṣpanepālyelāyutena tu/
SL1735/ tataḥ kṣaudraghṛtaṃ dattvā samyagbandhamathācaret//

SL1737/ pratisāraṇamekaikaṃ bhinne lagaṇa iṣyate//
SSu.6.14.6/ mahatyapi ca yuñjīta kṣārāgnī vidhikovidaḥ/
SL1739/ svinnāṃ bhinnāṃ viniṣpīḍya bhiṣagañjananāmikām//
SSu.6.14.7/ śilailānatasindhūtthaiḥ sakṣaudraiḥ pratisārayet/
SL1741/ rasāñjanamadhubhyāṃ tu bhittvā vā śastrakarmavit//
SSu.6.14.8/ pratisāryāñjanairyuñjyāduṣṇairdīpaśikhodbhavaiḥ/
SL1743/ samyaksvinne kṛmigranthau bhinne syāt pratisāraṇam//
SSu.6.14.9/ triphalātutthakāsīsasaindhavaiśca rasakriyā†/
SL1745/ bhittvopanāhaṃ kaphajaṃ pippalīmadhusaindhavaiḥ//
SSu.6.14.10/ lekhayenmaṇḍalāgreṇa samantāt pracchayedapi/
SL1747/ saṃsnehya patrabhaṅgaiśca svedayitvā yathāsukham//

SL1749/ saṃpakve prayato bhūtvā kurvīta vraṇaropaṇam//

<B 15>

pañcadaśo+adhyāyaḥ/
SSu.6.15.3/ snigdhaṃ bhuktavato hyannamupaviṣṭasya yatnataḥ/

SL1753/ arma yatra valījātaṃ tatraitallagayedbhiṣak//

SL1755/ mucuṇḍyā++ādāya medhāvī sūcīsūtreṇa vā punaḥ//

SL1757/ †śastrābādhabhayāccāsya vartmanī grāhayeddṛḍham//

SL1759/ ullikhanmaṇḍalāgreṇa tīkṣṇena pariśodhayet//
SSu.6.15.8/ vimuktaṃ sarvataścāpi kṛṣṇācchuklācca maṇḍalāt/
SL1761/ nītvā kanīnakopāntaṃ chindyānnātikanīnakam//
SSu.6.15.9/ caturbhāgasthite māṃse nākṣi vyāpattimṛcchati/
SL1763/ kanīnakavadhādasraṃ nāḍī vā+apyupajāyate//
SSu.6.15.10/ hīnacchedāt punarvṛddhiṃ śīgramevādhigacchati/
SL1765/ arma yajjālavadvyāpi tadapyunmārjya† lambitam//
SSu.6.15.11/ chindyādvakreṇa śastreṇa vartmaśuklāntamāthitam/
SL1767/ pratisāraṇamakṣṇostu tataḥ kāryamanantaram//
SSu.6.15.12/ yāvanālasya cūrṇena trikaṭorlavaṇasya ca/
SL1769/ svedayitvā tataḥ paścādbadhnīyāt kuśalo bhiṣak//
SSu.6.15.13/ doṣartubalakālajñaḥ snehaṃ dattvā yathāhitam/
SL1771/ vraṇavat saṃvidhānaṃ tu tasya kuryādataḥ param//
SSu.6.15.14/ tryahānmuktvā karasvedaṃ dattvā śodhanamācaret/
SL1773/ karañjabījāmalakamadhukaiḥ sādhitaṃ payaḥ//
SSu.6.15.15/ hitamāścyotanaṃ śūle dvirahnaḥ kṣaudrasaṃyutam/
SL1775/ madhukotpalakiñjalkadūrvākalkaiśca mūrdhani//
SSu.6.15.16/ pralepaḥ saghṛtaḥ śītaḥ kṣīrapiṣṭaḥ praśasyate/
SL1777/ lekhyāñjanairapaharedarmaśeṣaṃ bhavedyadi//
SSu.6.15.17/ arma cālpaṃ dadhinibhaṃ nīlaṃ raktamathāpi vā/
SL1779/ dhūsaraṃ tanu yaccāpi śukravattadupācaret//
SSu.6.15.18/ carmābhaṃ bahulaṃ yattu snāyumāṃsaghanāvṛtam/
SL1781/ chedyameva tadarma syāt kṛṣṇamaṇḍalagaṃ ca yat//
SSu.6.15.19/ viśuddhavarṇamakliṣṭaṃ kriyāsvakṣi gataklamam/
SL1783/ chinne+armaṇi bhavet samyagyathāsvamanupadravam//
SSu.6.15.20/ sirājāle sirā yāstu kaṭhināstāśca buddhimān/
SL1785/ ullikhenmaṇḍalāgreṇa baḍiśenāvalambitāḥ//
SSu.6.15.21/ sirāsu piḍakā jātā yā na sidhyanti bheṣajaiḥ/
SL1787/ armavanmaṇḍalāgreṇa tāsāṃ chedanamiṣyate//
SSu.6.15.22/ rogayoścaitayoḥ kāryamarmoktaṃ pratisāraṇam/
SL1789/ vidhiścāpi yathādoṣaṃ lekhanadravyasaṃbhṛtaḥ//
SSu.6.15.23/ sandhau saṃsvedya śastreṇa varṇīkāṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ/

SSu.6.15.24/ chindyāttato+ardhamagre syādaśrunāḍī hyato+anyathā/
SL1793/ pratisāraṇamatrāpi saindhavakṣaudramiṣyate//
SSu.6.15.25/ lekhanīyāni cūrṇāni vyādhiśeṣasya bheṣajam/
SL1795/ śaṅkhaṃ samudraphenaṃ ca maṇḍūkīṃ ca samudrajām//
SSu.6.15.26/ sphaṭikaṃ kuruvindaṃ ca pravālāśmantakaṃ tathā/
SL1797/ †vaidūryaṃ pulakaṃ muktāmayastāmrarajāṃsi ca//
SSu.6.15.27/ samabhāgāni saṃpiṣya sārdhaṃ srotoñjanena tu/
SL1799/ cūrṇāñjanaṃ kārayitvā bhājane bheṣaśṛṅgaje//
SSu.6.15.28/ saṃsthāpyobhayataḥ kālamañjayet satataṃ budhaḥ/
SL1801/ armāṇi piḍakāṃ hanyāt sirājālāni tena vai//
SSu.6.15.29/ arśastathā yacca nāmnā śuṣkārśo+arbudameva ca/
SL1803/ abhyantaraṃ vartmaśayā vidhānaṃ teṣu vakṣyate//
SSu.6.15.30/ vartmopasvedya nirbhujya sūcyotkṣipya prayatnataḥ/
SL1805/ maṇḍalāgreṇa tīkṣṇena mūle bhindyādbhiṣagvaraḥ//
SSu.6.15.31/ tataḥ saindhavakāsīsakṛṣṇābhiḥ pratisārayet/
SL1807/ sthite ca rudhire vartma dahet samyak śalākayā//
SSu.6.15.32/ kṣāreṇāvalikheccāpi vyādhiśeso bhavedyadi/
SL1809/ tīkṣṇairubhayatobhāgaistato doṣamadhikṣipet//
SSu.6.15.33/ vitarecca yathādoṣamabhiṣyandakriyāvidhim/
SL1811/ śastrakarmaṇyuparate māsaṃ ca syāt suyantritaḥ//

<B 16>

ṣoḍaśo+adhyāyaḥ/

SSu.6.16.8/ chittvā samaṃ vā+apyupapakṣmamālāṃ samyaggṛhītvā baḍiśaistribhistu/

SL1825/ virecanāścyotanadhūmanasyalepāñjanasneharasakriyāśca//

<B 17>

saptadaśo+adhyāyaḥ/

SL1925/ yantreṇotpīḍito doṣo nihanyādāśu darśanam//
SSu.6.17.53/ arāgi timiraṃ sādhyamādyaṃ paṭalamāśritam/
SL1927/ kṛcchraṃ dvitīye rāgi syāttṛtīye yāpyamucyate//

ekonaviṃśatitamo+adhyāyaḥ/
