SSu.6.21.29/ etā eva kriyāḥ kuryāt pittaghnaiḥ pittasaṃyute/
SL2348/ kākolyādau daśakṣīraṃ tiktaṃ cātra hitaṃ haviḥ//
SSu.6.21.30/ kṣīravṛkṣapravāleṣu madhuke candane tathā/
SL2350/ kalkakvāthe paraṃ pakvaṃ śarkarāmadhukaiḥ saraiḥ//
SSu.6.21.31/ iṅgudīsarṣapasnehau sakaphe pūraṇe hitau/
SL2352/ tiktauṣadhānāṃ yūṣāśca svedāśca kaphanāśanāḥ//
SSu.6.21.32/ surasādau kṛtaṃ tailaṃ pañcamūle mahatyapi/
SL2354/ mātuluṅgarasaḥ śuktaṃ laśunārdrakayo rasaḥ//
SSu.6.21.33/ ekaikaḥ pūraṇe pathyastailaṃ teṣvapi vā kṛtam/
SL2356/ tīkṣṇā mūrdhavirekāśca kavalāścātra pūjitāḥ//
SSu.6.21.34/ karṇaśūlavidhiḥ kṛtsnaḥ pittaghnaḥ śoṇitāvṛte/
SL2358/ śūlapraṇādabādhiryakṣveḍānāṃ tu prakīrtitam//
SSu.6.21.35/ sāmānyato viśeṣeṇa† bādhirye pūraṇaṃ śṛṇu/
SL2360/ gavāṃ mūtreṇa bilvāni piṣṭvā tailaṃ vipācayet//
SSu.6.21.36/ sajalaṃ ca sadugdhaṃ ca bādhirye karṇapūraṇam/
SL2362/ sitāmadhukabimbībhiḥ siddhaṃ vā++āje payasyapi//
SSu.6.21.37/ bimbīkvāthe vimathyoṣṇaṃ śītībhūtaṃ taduddhṛtam/
SL2364/ punaḥ paceddaśakṣīraṃ sitāmadhukacandanaiḥ//
SSu.6.21.38/ bilvāmbugāḍhaṃ tattailaṃ bādhirye karṇapūraṇam/
SL2366/ vakṣyate yaḥ pratiśyāye vidhiḥ so+apyatra pūjitaḥ//
SSu.6.21.39/ vātavyādhiṣu yaścokto vidhiḥ sa ca hito bhavet/
SL2368/ karṇasrāve pūtikarṇe tathaiva kṛmikarṇake//
SSu.6.21.40/ samānaṃ karma kurvīta yogān vaiśeṣikānapi/
SL2370/ śirovirecanaṃ caiva dhūpanaṃ pūraṇaṃ tathā//
SSu.6.21.41/ pramārjanaṃ dhāvanaṃ ca vīkṣya vīkṣyāvcārayet/
SL2372/ rājavṛkṣāditoyena surasādigaṇena vā//
SSu.6.21.42/ karṇaprakṣālanaṃ kāryaṃ cūrṇaireṣāṃ ca pūraṇam/
SL2374/ kvāthaṃ pañjcakaṣāyaṃ tu kapittharasayojitam//
SSu.6.21.43/ karṇasrāve praśaṃsanti pūraṇaṃ madhunā saha/
SL2376/ sarjatvakcūrṇasaṃyuktaḥ kārpāsīphalajo rasaḥ//
SSu.6.21.44/ yojito madhunā vā+api karṇasrāve praśasyate/
SL2378/ lākṣā rasāñjanaṃ sarjaścūrṇitaṃ karṇapūraṇam//
SSu.6.21.45/ saśaivalaṃ mahāvṛkṣajambvāmraprasavāyutam/
SL2380/ kulīrakṣaudramaṇḍūkīsiddhaṃ tailaṃ ca pūjitam//
SSu.6.21.46/ tindukānyabhayā rodhraṃ samaṅgā++āmalakaṃ madhu/
SL2382/ pūraṇaṃ cātra† pathyaṃ syātkapittharasayojitam//
SSu.6.21.47/ rasamāmrakapitthānāṃ madhūkadhavaśālajam/
SL2384/ pūraṇārthaṃ praśaṃsanti tailaṃ vā tairvipācitam//
SSu.6.21.48/ priyaṅgumadhukāmbaṣṭhādhātakīśilaparṇibhiḥ/
SL2386/ mañjiṣṭhālobhralākṣābhiḥ kapitthasya rasena vā//
SSu.6.21.49/ pacettailaṃ tadāsrāvamavagṛhṇāti pūraṇāt/
SL2388/ ghṛtaṃ rasāñjnaṃ nāryāḥ kṣīreṇa madhusaṃyutam//
SSu.6.21.50/ tatpraśastaṃ cirotthe+api sāsrāve pūtikarṇake/
SL2390/ nirguṇḍīsvarasastailaṃ sindhurdhūmarajo guḍaḥ//
SSu.6.21.51/ pūraṇaḥ pūtikarṇasya śamano madhusaṃyutaḥ/
SL2392/ kṛmikarṇakanāśārthaṃ kṛmighnaṃ yojayedvidhim//
SSu.6.21.52/ vārtākudhūmaśca hitaḥ sārṣapasneha eva ca/
SL2394/ kṛmighnaṃ haritālena gavāṃ mūtrayutena ca//
SSu.6.21.53/ gugguloḥ karṇadaurgandhye dhūpanaṃ śreṣṭhamucyate/
SL2396/ chardanaṃ dhūmapānaṃ ca kavalasya ca dhāraṇam//
SSu.6.21.54/ karṇakṣveḍe hitaṃ tailaṃ sārṣapaṃ caiva pūraṇam/
SL2398/ vidradhau cāpi kurvīta vidradhyuktaṃ cikitsitam//
SSu.6.21.55/ prakledya dhīmāṃstailena svedena pravilāyya ca/
SL2400/ śodhayetkarṇaviṭkaṃ tu bhiṣak samyak śalākayā//
SSu.6.21.56/ nāḍīsvedo+atha vamanaṃ dhūmo mūrdhavirecanam/
SL2402/ vidhiśca kaphahṛtsarvaḥ karṇakaṇḍūmapohati//
SSu.6.21.57/ atha karṇapratīnāhe snehasvedau prayojayet/
SL2404/ tato viriktaśirasaḥ kriyāṃ prāptāṃ samācaret//
SSu.6.21.58/ karṇapākasya bhaiṣajyaṃ kuryātpittavisarpavat/
SL2406/ karṇacchidre vartamānaṃ kīṭaṃ kledamalādi vā//
SSu.6.21.59/ śṛṅgeṇāpahareddhīmānathavā+api śalākayā/
SL2408/ śeṣāṇāṃ tu vikārāṇāṃ prāk cikitsitamīritam//

<B 22>

dvāviṃśatitamo+adhyāyaḥ//
SSu.6.22.3/ apīnasaḥ pūtinasyaṃ† nāsāpākastathaiva ca/
SL2410/ tathā śoṇitapittaṃ ca pūyaśoṇitameva ca//
SSu.6.22.4/ kṣavathurbhraṃśathurdīpto nāsānāhaḥ parisravaḥ/
SL2412/ nāsāśoṣeṇa sahitā daśaikāśceritā gadāḥ//
SSu.6.22.5/ catvāryarśāṃsi catvāraḥ śophāḥ saptārbudāni ca/
SL2414/ pratiśyāyāśca ye pañca vakṣyante sacikitsitāḥ/
SL2415/ †ekatriṃśanmitāste tu nāsārogāḥ prakīrtitāḥ//
SSu.6.22.6/ ānahyate yasya vidhūpyate ca praklidyate śuṣyati† cāpi nāsā/
SL2417/ na vetti yo gandharasāṃśca janturjuṣṭaṃ vyavasyettamapīnasena//
SSu.6.22.7/ taṃ cānilaśleṣmabhavaṃ vikāraṃ brūyāt pratiśyāyasamānaliṅgam/
SL2419/ doṣairvidagdhairlatālumūle saṃvāsito yasya samīraṇastu//
SSu.6.22.8/ nireti pūtirmukhanāsikābhyāṃ taṃ pūtināsaṃ pravadanti rogam/
SL2421/ vrāṇāśritaṃ pittamarūṃṣi kuryādyasmin vikāre balavāṃśca pākaḥ//
SSu.6.22.9/ taṃ nāsikāpākamiti vyavasyedvikledakothāvapi yatra dṛṣṭau/
SL2423/ caturvidhaṃ dviprabhavaṃ vakṣyāmi bhūyaḥ khalu raktapittam//
SSu.6.22.10/ doṣairvidagdhairathavā+api jantorlalāṭadeśe+abhihatasya taistu/
SL2425/ nāsā sravet pūyamasṛgvimiśraṃ taṃ pūyaraktaṃ pravadanti rogam//
SSu.6.22.11/ ghrāṇāśrite marmaṇi saṃpraduṣṭe† yasyānilo nāsikayā nireti//
SSu.6.22.12/ kaphānuyāto bahuśaḥ saśabdastaṃ rogamāhuḥ kṣavathuṃ vidhijñāḥ/
SL2428/ tīkṣṇopayogādatijighrato vā bhāvān kaṭūnarkanirīkṣaṇādvā//
SSu.6.22.13/ sūtrādibhirvā taruṇāsthimarmaṇyudghāṭite+anyaḥ kṣavathurnireti/
SL2430/ prabhraśyate nāsikayaiva yaśca† sāndro vidagdho lavaṇaḥ kaphastu//
SSu.6.22.14/ prāk saṃcito mūrdhani pittataptastaṃ bhraṃśathuṃ vyādhimudāharanti/
SL2432/ ghrāṇe bhṛśaṃ dāhasamanvite tu viniḥsareddhūma iveha vāyuḥ//
SSu.6.22.15/ nāsā pradīpteva ca yasya jantorvyādhiṃ tu taṃ dīptamudāharanti/
SL2434/ kaphāvṛto vāyurudānasaṃjño yadā svamārge viguṇaḥ sthitaḥ syāt//
SSu.6.22.16/ ghrāṇaṃ vṛṇotīva tadā sa rogo nāsāpratīnāha iti pradiṣṭaḥ/
SL2436/ ajasramacchaṃ salilaprakāśaṃ yasyāvivarṇe sravatīha nāsā//
SSu.6.22.17/ rātrau viśeṣeṇa hi taṃ vikāraṃ nāsāparisrāvamiti vyavasyet/
SL2438/ ghrāṇāśrite śleṣmaṇi mārutena pittena gāḍhaṃ pariśoṣite ca//
SSu.6.22.18/ samucchvasityūrdhvamadhaśca kṛcchrādyastasya nāsāpariśoṣa uktaḥ/
SL2440/ doṣaiśtribhistaiḥ pṛthagekaśaśca brūyāttathā+arśāṃsi tathaiva śophān//
SSu.6.22.19/ śālākyasiddhāntamavekṣya cāpi sarvātmakaṃ saptamamarbudaṃ tu/
SL2442/ rogaḥ pratiśyāya ihopadiṣṭaḥ sa vakṣyate pañcavidhaḥ purastāt//
SSu.6.22.20/ (nāsāsrotogatā rogāstriṃśadekaśca kīrtitāḥ/
SL2444/ srotaḥpathe yadvipulaṃ kośavaccārbudaṃ bhavet†//)
SSu.6.22.21/ śophāstu śophavijñānā nāsāsrotovyavasthitāḥ/
SL2446/ nidāne+arśāṃsi nirdiṣṭānyevaṃ tāni vibhāvayet//

<B 23>

trayoviṃśatitamo+adhyāyaḥ/
SSu.6.23.3/ pūrvoddiṣṭe pūtinasye ca jantoḥ snehasvedau chardanaṃ sraṃsanaṃ ca/
SL2448/ yuktaṃ bhaktaṃ tīkṣṇamalpaṃ laghu syāduṣṇaṃ toyaṃ dhūmapānaṃ ca kāle//

SL2452/ nāsāpāke pittahṛtsaṃvidhānaṃ kāryaṃ sarvaṃ bāhyamābhyantaraṃ ca//

SL2454/ vakṣyāmyūrdhvaṃ raktapītopaśāntiṃ nāḍīvatsyāt pūyarakte cikitsā//
SSu.6.23.7/ vānte samyak cāvapīḍaṃ vadanti tīkṣṇaṃ dhūmaṃ śodhanaṃ cātra nasyam/
SL2456/ kṣepyaṃ nasyaṃ mūrdhavairecanīyairnāḍyā cūrṇaṃ kṣavathau bhraṃśathau ca//
SSu.6.23.8/ kuryāt svedān mūrdhni vātāmayaghnān snigdhān dhūmān yadyanyaddhitaṃ ca/
SL2458/ dīpte roge paittikaṃ saṃvidhānaṃ kuryāt sarvaṃ svādu yacchītalaṃ ca//
SSu.6.23.9/ nāsānāhe snehapānaṃ pradhānaṃ snigdhā dhūmā mūrdhabastiśca nityam/
SL2460/ balātailaṃ sarvathaivopayojyaṃ vātavyādhāvanyaduktaṃ ca yadyat//
SSu.6.23.10/ nāsāsrāve ghrāṇataścūrṇamuktaṃ nāḍyā deyaṃ yo+avapīḍaśca tīkṣṇaḥ/
SL2462/ tīkṣṇaṃ dhūmaṃ devadārvagnikābhyāṃ māṃsaṃ vā++ājaṃ yuktamatrādiśanti//
SSu.6.23.11/ nāsāśoṣe kṣīrasarpiḥ pradhānaṃ siddhaṃ tailaṃ cāṇukalpena nasyam/
SL2464/ sarpiḥpānaṃ bhojanaṃ jāṅgalaiśca snehaḥ svedaḥ snaihikaścāpi dhūmaḥ//
SSu.6.23.12/ śeṣān rogān ghrāṇajān sanniyaccheduktaṃ teṣāṃ yadyathā saṃvidhānam//

<B 24>

caturviṃśatitamo+adhyāyaḥ/
SSu.6.24.3/ nārīprasaṅgaḥ śiraso+abhitāpo dhūmo rajaḥ śītamatipratāpaḥ/
SL2467/ saṃdhāraṇaṃ mūtrapurīṣayośca sadyaḥ pratiśyāyanidānamuktam//

SL2475/ uṣṇaḥ sapītakaḥ srāvo ghrāṇāt sravati paittike//

SL2477/ sadhūmaṃ sahasā vahniṃ vamatīva ca mānavaḥ//

SL2481/ bhūtvā bhūtvā pratiśyāyo yo+akasmādvinivartate//

SL2483/ liṅgāni caiva sarveṣāṃ pīnasānāṃ ca sarvaje//

SL2495/ bādhiryamāndhyamaghrāṇaṃ ghorāṃśca nayanāmayān/

SL2498/ svedairvicitrairvamanaiśca yuktaiḥ kālopapannairavapīḍanaiśca//

SL2533/ tailaṃ vipakvaṃ nasyārthe vidadhyāccātra buddhimān//
SSu.6.24.36/ mustā tejovatī pāṭhā kaṭphalaṃ kaṭukā vacā/
SL2535/ sarṣapāḥ pippalīmūlaṃ pippalyaḥ saindhavāgnikau//
SSu.6.24.37/ tutthaṃ karañjabījaṃ ca lavaṇaṃ bhadradāru ca/
SL2537/ etaiḥ kṛtaṃ kaṣāyaṃ tu kavale saṃprayojayet//
SSu.6.24.38/ hitaṃ mūrdhavireke ca tailamebhirvipācitam/
SL2539/ kṣīramardhajale kvāthyaṃ jāṅgalairmṛgapakṣibhiḥ//
SSu.6.24.39/ puṣpairvimiśraṃ jalajairvātaghnairauṣadhairapi/
SL2541/ hime kṣīrāvaśiṣṭe+asmin ghṛtamutpādya yatnataḥ//
SSu.6.24.40/ sarvagandhasitānantāmadhukaṃ† candanaṃ tathā/
SL2543/ āvāpya vipacedbhūyo daśakṣīraṃ tu tadghṛtam//
SSu.6.24.41/ nasye prayuktamudriktān pratiśyāyān vyapohati/
SL2545/ yathāsvaṃ doṣaśamanaistailaṃ kuryācca yatnataḥ//

<B 25>

pañcaviṃśatitamo+adhyāyaḥ/
SSu.6.25.1/ athātaḥ śirorogavijñānīyamadhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ//

SL2569/ śītena śāntiṃ labhate kadāciduṣṇena jantuḥ sukhamāpnuyācca//
SSu.6.25.13/ taṃ bhāskarāvartamudāharanti sarvātmakaṃ kaṣṭatamaṃ vikāram/
SL2571/ doṣāstu duṣṭāstraya eva manyāṃ saṃpīḍya ghāṭāsu rujāṃ sutīvrām//
SSu.6.25.14/ kurvanti sākṣibhruvi śaṅkhadeśe sthitiṃ karotyāśu viśeṣatastu/
SL2573/ gaṇḍasya pārśve tu karoti kampaṃ hanugrahaṃ locanajāṃśca rogān//
SSu.6.25.15/ anantavātaṃ tamudāharanti doṣatrayotthaṃ śiraso vikāram/
SL2575/ yasyottamāṅgārdhamatīva jantoḥ saṃbhedatodabhramaśūlajuṣṭam//
SSu.6.25.16/ pakṣāddaśāhādathavā+apyakasmāttasyārdhamedaṃ tritayādvyavasyet/
SL2577/ śaṅkhāśrito vāyurudīrṇavegaḥ kṛtānuyātraḥ kaphapittaraktaiḥ//

saptaviṃśatitamo+adhyāyaḥ/

SL2690/ durvarṇaḥ satatamadhaḥśayo+amlagandhistaṃ brūyurbhiṣaja ihāndhapūtanārtam//
SSu.6.27.14/ udvigro bhṛśamativepate prarudyāt saṃlīnaḥ ssvapiti ca yasya cāntrakūjaḥ/
SL2692/ visrāṅgo bhṛśamatisāryate ca yastaṃ jānīyādbhiṣagiha śītapūtanārtam//

<B 30>

triṃśattamo+adhyāyaḥ/
SSu.6.30.3/ śakunyabhiparītasya kāryo vaidyena jānatā/

SL2755/ tilataṇḍulakaṃ mālyaṃ haritālaṃ manaḥśilā//

SL2757/ niṣkuṭe ca prayoktavyaṃ snānamasya yathāvidhi//

SL2759/ kuryācca vividhāṃ pūjāṃ śakunyāḥ kusumaiḥ śubhaiḥ//

SL2761/ ayomukhī tīkṣṇatuṇḍā śakunī te prasīdatu//

SL2763/ lambodarī †śaṅkukarṇī śakunī te prasīdatu//

aṣṭatriṃśattamo+adhyāyaḥ/

<B 39>

ekonacatvāriṃśattamo+adhyāyaḥ/
SSu.6.39.3/ yenāmṛtamapāṃ madhyāduddhṛtaṃ pūrvajanmani/

SL4049/ arkāmṛtākṣārajaloṣitebhyaḥ kṛtvā yavebhyo vividhāṃśca bhakṣyān//
SSu.6.41.39/ khādet pibet sarpirajāvikaṃ vā kṛśo yavāgvā saha bhaktakāle/
SL4051/ sarpirmadhubhyāṃ trikaṭu pralihyāccavyāviḍaṅgopahitaṃ kṣayārtaḥ//
SSu.6.41.40/ māṃsādamāṃseṣu ghṛtaṃ ca siddhaṃ śosāpahaṃ kṣaudrakaṇāsametam/
SL4053/ drākṣāsitāmāgadhikāvalehaḥ sakṣaudratailaḥ kṣayarogaghātī//
SSu.6.41.41/ ghṛtena cājena samākṣikeṇa turaṅgagandhātilamāṣacūrṇam/
SL4055/ sitāśvagandhāmagadhodbhavānāṃ †cūrṇaṃ ghṛtakṣaudrayutaṃ pralihyāt//

SL4065/ dve pañcamūlyau varuṇaṃ karañjaṃ bhallātakaṃ bilvapunarnave ca//
SSu.6.41.47/ yavān kulatthān badarāṇi bhārgīṃ pāṭhāṃ hutāśaṃ samahīkadambam/

SL4069/ etaddhi śoṣaṃ jaṭharāṇi caiva hanyāt pramehāṃśca sahānilena//

SL4073/ viḍaṅgabhallātakacitrakogrākaṭutrikāmbhodasurāṣṭrajāṃśca//

SL4075/ triṃśatpalānyatra sitopalāyā dattvā tugākṣīripalāni ṣaṭ ca//
SSu.6.41.52/ prasthe ghṛtasya dviguṇaṃ ca dadyāt kṣaudraṃ tato manthahataṃ vidadhyāt/
SL4077/ palaṃ palaṃ prātarataḥ pralihya paścāt pibet kṣīramatandritaśca//

SL4079/ yakṣmāṇamāśu vyapahanti caitat pāṇḍvāmayaṃ caiva bhagandaraṃ ca//

SL4083/ upadravāṃśca svaravaikṛtādīn jayedyathāsvaṃ prasamīkṣya śāstram//

SL4085/ snānādinānāvidhinā jahāti māsādaśeṣaṃ niyamena śoṣam//
SSu.6.41.57/ rasonayogaṃ vidhivat kṣayārtaḥ kṣīreṇa vā nāgabalāprayogam/
SL4087/ seveta vā māgadhikāvidhānaṃ tathopayogaṃ jatuno+aśmajasya//
SSu.6.41.58/ śokaṃ striyaṃ krodhamasūyanaṃ ca tyajedudārān viṣayān bhajeta/
SL4089/ vaidyān dvijātīṃstridaśān gurūśca vācaśca puṇyāḥ śṛṇuyādbijebhyaḥ//

dvicatvāriṃśattamo+adhyāyaḥ/

SSu.6.42.107/ rasān seveta pittaghnān pittalāni vivarjayet/
SL4299/ pālāśaṃ dhānvanaṃ vā+api pibedyūṣaṃ saśarkaram//

SSu.6.42.138/ sarvatra vardhate kṣipraṃ bhramannatha† saghoṣavān/

SSu.6.44.13/ upadravāsteṣvaruciḥ pipāsā chardirjvaro mūrdharujā+agnisādaḥ/

SL4443/ khādedguḍaṃ vā+apyabhayāvipakvamāragvadhādikvathitaṃ† pibedvā//

SSu.6.44.18/ ahrecca doṣān bahuśo+alpamātrān śvayeddhi doṣeṣvatinirhṛteṣu/

SSu.6.44.19/ ubhe bṛhatyau rajanīṃ śukākhyāṃ kākādanīṃ cāpi sakākamācīm/

SL4451/ hitaṃ ca yaṣṭīmadhujaṃ† kaṣāyaṃ cūrṇaṃ samaṃ vā madhunā+avalihyāt//
SSu.6.44.21/ gomūtrayuktaṃ triphalādalānāṃ dattvā++āyasaṃ cūrṇamanalpakālam/
SL4453/ pravālamuktāñjanaśaṅkhacūrṇaṃ lihyāttathā kāñcanagairikottham//
SSu.6.44.22/ ājaṃ śakṛtsyāt† kuḍavapramāṇaṃ viḍaṃ haridrā lavaṇottamaṃ ca/

SSu.6.48.14/ rasakṣayoktāni ca lakṣaṇāni tasyāmaśeṣeṇa bhiṣagvyavasyet/

SL4811/ gaṇḍūṣamamlairvirase ca vaktre kuryācchubhairāmalakasya cūrṇaiḥ//
SSu.6.48.18/ suvarṇarūpyādibhiragnitaptairloṣṭaiḥ kṛtaṃ vā sikatādibhirvā†/
SL4813/ jalaṃ sukhoṣṇaṃ śamayettu tṛṣṇāṃ saśarkaraṃ kṣaudrayutaṃ himaṃ vā//

tripañcāśattamo+adhyāyaḥ/

SL5190/ śatāvarīcūrṇayogaṃ balācūrṇamathāpi vā//
SSu.6.53.15/ pibet kaṭūni mūtreṇa kaphaje svarasaṃkṣaye/

pañcapañcāśattamo+adhyāyaḥ/

SL5396/ pakve tato+anne tu vilaṅghanaṃ syāt saṃpācanaṃ cāpi virecanaṃ ca//
SSu.6.56.13/ viśuddhadehasya hi sadya eva mūrcchātisārādirupaiti śāntim/
SL5398/ āsthāpanaṃ cāpi vadanti pathyaṃ sarvāsu yogānaparānnibodha//
SSu.6.56.14/ pathyāvacāhiṅgukaliṅgagṛñjasauvarcalaiḥ sātiviṣaiśca cūrṇam/
SL5400/ sukhāmbupītaṃ vinihantyajīrṇaṃ śūlaṃ visūcīmaruciṃ ca sadyaḥ//
SSu.6.56.15/ kṣārāgadaṃ vā lavaṇaṃ viṣaṃ vā guḍapragāḍhānatha sarṣapān vā/
SL5402/ amlena vā saindhavahiṅguyuktau sabījapūrṇau saghṛtau trivargau//
SSu.6.56.16/ kaṭutrikaṃ vā lavaṇairupetaṃ pibet snuhīkṣīravimiśritaṃ tu/
SL5404/ kalyāṇakaṃ vā lavaṇaṃ pibettu yaduktamādāvanilāmayeṣu//
SSu.6.56.17/ kṛṣṇājamodakṣavakāṇi vā+api tulyau pibedvā magadhānikumbhau/
SL5406/ dantīyuta vā magadhodbhavānāṃ kalkaṃ pibet koṣavatīrasena//
SSu.6.56.18/ uṣṇābhiradbhirmagadhodbhavānāṃ kalkaṃ pibennāgarakalkayuktam/
SL5408/ vyoṣaṃ karañjasya phalaṃ haridre mūlaṃ samaṃ cāpyatha mātuluṅgyāḥ//
SSu.6.56.19/ chāyāviśuṣkā guṭikāḥ kṛtāstā hanyurvisūcīṃ nayanāñjanena/
SL5410/ suvāmitaṃ sādhuvirecitaṃ vā sulaṅghataṃ vā manujaṃ viditvā//
SSu.6.56.20/ peyādibhirdīpanapācanīyaiḥ samyakkṣudhārtaṃ samupakrameta/
SL5412/ āmaṃ śakṛdvā nicitaṃ krameṇa bhūyo vibaddhaṃ viguṇānilena//
SSu.6.56.21/ pravartamānaṃ na yathāsvamenaṃ vikāramānāhamudāharanti/
SL5414/ tasmin bhavantyāmasamudbhave tu tṛṣṇāpratiśyāyaśirovidāhāḥ//
SSu.6.56.22/ āmāśaye śūlamatho gurutvaṃ hṛllāsa udgāravighātanaṃ ca/
SL5416/ stambhaḥ kaṭīpṛṣṭhapurīṣamūtre śūlo+atha mūrcchā †sa śakṛdvamecca//
SSu.6.56.23/ śvāsaśca pakvāśayaje bhavanti liṅgāni cātrālasakodbhavāni/
SL5418/ āmodbhave vāntamupakrameta saṃsargabhaktakramadīpanīyaiḥ//
SSu.6.56.24/ athetaraṃ yo na śakṛdvamettamāmaṃ jayet svedanapācanaiśca/
SL5420/ visūcikāyāṃ parikīrtitāni dravyāṇi vairecanikāni yāni//
SSu.6.56.25/ tānyeva vartīrviracedvicūrṇya mahiṣyajāvībhagavāṃ tu mūtraiḥ/
SL5422/ svinnasya pāyau viniveśya tāśca cūrṇāni caiṣāṃ pradhamettu nāḍyā//
SSu.6.56.26/ mūtreṣu saṃsādhya yathāvidhānaṃ dravyāṇi yānyūrdhvamadhaśca yānti/
SL5424/ kvāthena tenāśu nirūhayecca mūtrārdhayuktena samākṣikeṇa//
SSu.6.56.27/ tribhaṇḍiyuktaṃ lavaṇaprakuñcaṃ dattvā viriktakramamācarecca/
SL5426/ eṣveva tailena ca sādhitena prāptaṃ yadi syādanuvāsayecca//

saptapañcāśattamo+adhyāyaḥ/

<B 59>

ūnaṣaṣṭitamo+adhyāyaḥ/
SSu.6.59.3/ vātena pittena kaphena sarvaistathā+ābhighātaiḥ śakṛdaśmarībhyām/
SL5600/ tathā+aparaḥ śarkarayā sukaṣṭo mūtropaghātaḥ kathito+aṣṭamastu//
SSu.6.59.4/ alpamalpaṃ samutpīḍya muṣkamehanabastibhiḥ/
SL5602/ phaladbhiriva kṛcchreṇa vātāghātena mehati//
SSu.6.59.5/ hāridramuṣṇaṃ raktaṃ vā muṣkamehanabastibhiḥ/
SL5604/ agninā dahyamānābhaiḥ pittāghātena mehati//
SSu.6.59.6/ snigdhaṃ śuklamanuṣṇaṃ ca muṣkamehanabastibhiḥ/
SL5606/ saṃhṛṣṭaromā gurubhiḥ śleṣmāghātena mehati//
SSu.6.59.7/ dāhaśītarujāviṣṭo nānāvarṇaṃ muhurmuhuḥ/
SL5608/ tāmyamānastu kṛcchreṇa sannipātena mehati//
SSu.6.59.8/ mūtravāhiṣu śalyena kṣateṣvabhihateṣu ca/
SL5610/ srotaḥsu mūtrāghātastu jāyate bhṛśavedanaḥ†//
SSu.6.59.9/ vātabastestu tulyāni tasya liṅgāni lakṣayet/
SL5612/ śakṛtastu pratīghātādvāyurviguṇatāṃ gataḥ//
SSu.6.59.10/ ādhmānaṃ ca saśūlaṃ ca mūtrasaṅgaṃ karoti hi/
SL5614/ aśmarīhetukaḥ pūrvaṃ mūtraghāta udāhṛtaḥ//
SSu.6.59.11/ aśmarī śarkarā caiva †tulye saṃbhavalakṣaṇaiḥ/
SL5616/ śarkarāyā viśeṣaṃ tu śṛṇu kīrtayato mama//
SSu.6.59.12/ pacyamānasya pittena bhidyamānasya vāyunā/
SL5618/ śleṣmaṇo+avayavā bhinnāḥ śarkarā iti saṃjñitāḥ//
SSu.6.59.13/ hṛtpīḍā vepathuḥ śūlaṃ kukṣau† vahniḥ sudurbalaḥ/
SL5620/ tābhirbhavati mūrcchā ca mūtrāghātaśca dāruṇaḥ//
SSu.6.59.14/ mūtraveganirastāsu tāsu śāmyati vedanā/
SL5622/ †yāvadanyā punarnaiti guḍikā srotaso mukham//
SSu.6.59.15/ śarkarāsaṃbhavasyaitanmūtrāghātasya lakṣaṇam/
SL5624/ cikitsitamathaiteṣāmaṣṭānāmapi vakṣyate//
SSu.6.59.16/ aśmarīṃ ca samāśritya yaduktaṃ prasamīkṣya tat/
SL5626/ yathādoṣaṃ prayuñjīta snehādimapi ca kramam//
SSu.6.59.17/ śvadaṃṣṭrāśmabhidau kumbhīṃ hapuṣāṃ kaṇṭakārikām/
SL5628/ balāṃ śatāvarīṃ rāsnāṃ varuṇaṃ girikarṇikām//
SSu.6.59.18/ tathā vidārigandhādiṃ saṃhṛtya traivṛtaṃ pacet/
SL5630/ tailaṃ ghṛtaṃ vā tat peyaṃ tena vā+apyanuvāsanam//
SSu.6.59.19/ dadyāduttarabastiṃ ca vātakṛcchropaśāntaye/
SL5632/ śvadaṃṣṭrāsvarase tailaṃ saguḍakṣīranāgaram//
SSu.6.59.20/ paktvā tat pūrvavadyojyaṃ tatrānilarujāpaham/
SL5634/ tṛṇotpalādikākolīnyagrodhādigaṇaiḥ kṛtam(śṛtam)//
SSu.6.59.21/ pītaṃ ghṛtaṃ pittakṛcchraṃ nāśayet kṣīrameva vā/
SL5636/ dadyāduttarabastiṃ ca pittakṛcchropaśāntaye//
SSu.6.59.22/ ebhireva kṛtaḥ snehastrividheṣvapi bastiṣu/
SL5638/ hitaṃ virecanaṃ cekṣukṣīradrākṣārasairyutam//
SSu.6.59.23/ surasoṣakamustādau varuṇādau ca †yat kṛtam/
SL5640/ tailaṃ tathā yavāgvādi †kaphāghāte praśasyate//
SSu.6.59.24/ yathādoṣocchrayaṃ kuryādetāneva ca sarvaje/
SL5642/ phalguvṛścīradarbhāśmasāracūrṇaṃ ca vāriṇā//
SSu.6.59.25/ surekṣurasadarbhāmbupītaṃ kṛcchrarujāpaham/
SL5644/ tathā+abhighātaje kuryāt sadyovraṇacikitsitam//
SSu.6.59.26/ mūtrakṛcchre śakṛjjāte kāryā vātaharī kriyā/
SL5646/ svedāvagāhāvabhyaṅgabasticūrṇakriyāstathā//
SSu.6.59.27/ ye tvanye tu tathā kṛcchre tayoḥ proktaḥ kriyāvidhiḥ//

<B 60>

ṣaṣṭitamo+adhyāyaḥ/
SSu.6.60.3/ niśācarebhyo rakṣyastu nityameva kṣatāturaḥ/
SL5649/ iti yat prāgabhihitaṃ vistarastasya vakṣyate//
SSu.6.60.4/ guhyānāgatavijñānamanavasthā+asahiṣṇutā/
SL5651/ kriyā vā+amānuṣī yasmin sagrahaḥ parikīrtyate//
SSu.6.60.5/ aśuciṃ bhinnamaryādaṃ kṣataṃ vā yadi vā+akṣatam/
SL5653/ hiṃsyurhiṃsāvihārārthaṃ satkārārthamathāpi vā//
SSu.6.60.6/ asaṅkhyeyā grahagaṇā grahādhipatayastu ye/
SL5655/ vyajyante vividhākārā bhidyante te tathā+aṣṭadhā//
SSu.6.60.7/ devāstathā śatrugaṇāśca teṣāṃ gandharvayakṣāḥ pitaro bhujaṅgāḥ/
SL5657/ rakṣāṃsi yā cāpi piśācajātireṣo+aṣṭako devagaṇo grahākhyaḥ//
SSu.6.60.8/ saṃtuṣṭaḥ śucirapi ceṣṭagandhamālyo nistandrī hyavitathasaṃskṛtaprabhāṣī/
SL5659/ tejasvī sthiranayano varapradātā brahmaṇyo bhavati naraḥ sa devajuṣṭaḥ//
SSu.6.60.9/ saṃsvedī dvijagurudevadoṣavaktā jihmākṣo vigatabhayo vimārgadṛṣṭiḥ/
SL5661/ santuṣṭo bhavati na cānnapānajātairduṣṭātmā bhavati ca devaśatrujuṣṭaḥ//
SSu.6.60.10/ hṛṣṭātmā pulinavanāntaropasevī svācāraḥ priyaparigītagandhamālyaḥ/
SL5663/ nṛtyan vai prahasati cāru cālpaśabdaṃ gandharvagrahaparipīḍito manuṣyaḥ//
SSu.6.60.11/ tāmrākṣaḥ priyatanuraktavastradhārī gambhīro †drutamatiralpavāk sahiṣśuḥ/
SL5665/ tejasvī vadati ca kiṃ dadāmi kasmai yo yakṣagrahaparipīḍito manuṣyaḥ//
SSu.6.60.12/ pretebhyo visṛjati saṃstareṣu piṇḍān śāntātmā jalamapi cāpasavyavastraḥ/
SL5667/ māṃsepsustilaguḍapāyasābhikāmastadbhukto† bhavati pitṛgrahābhibhūtaḥ//
SSu.6.60.13/ bhūmau yaḥ prasarati sarpavat kadācit sṛkkiṇyau vilikhati† jihvayā tathaiva/
SL5669/ nidrālurguḍamadhudugdhapāyasepsurvijñeyo bhavati bhujaṅgamena juṣṭaḥ//
SSu.6.60.14/ māṃsāsṛgvividhasurāvikāralipsurnirlajjo bhṛśamatiniṣṭhuro+atiśūraḥ/
SL5671/ krodhālurvipulabalo niśāvihārī śaucadviḍ bhavati ca rakṣasā gṛhītaḥ//
SSu.6.60.15/ uddhastaḥ kṛśaparuṣaścirapralāpī durgandho bhṛśamaśucistathā+atilolaḥ/
SL5673/ bahvāśī vijanahimāmburātrisevī †vyāvigno bhramati rudan piśācajuṣṭaḥ//
SSu.6.60.16/ sthūlākṣastvaritagatiḥ svaphenalehī nidrāluḥ patati ca kampate ca yo+ati/
SL5675/ yaścādridviradanagādivicyutaḥ san saṃsṛṣṭo na bhavati vārdhakena juṣṭaḥ//
SSu.6.60.17/ devagrahāḥ paurṇamāsyāmasurāḥ sandhyayorapi/
SL5677/ gandharvāḥ prāyaśo+aṣṭamyāṃ yakṣāśca pratipadyatha//
SSu.6.60.18/ kṛṣṇakṣaye ca pitaraḥ paścamyāmapi coragāḥ/
SL5679/ rakṣāṃsi niśi paiśācāścaturdaśyāṃ viśanti ca//
SSu.6.60.19/ darpaṇādīn yathā chāyā śītoṣṇaṃ prāṇino yathā/
SL5681/ svamaṇiṃ bhāskarasyosrā yathā dehaṃ ca dehadhṛk/
SL5682/ viśanti ca na dṛśyante grahāstadvaccharīriṇam//
SSu.6.60.20/ tapāṃsi tīvrāṇi tathaiva dānaṃ vratāni dharmo niyamāśca satyam/

SL5686/ ye tvāviśantīti vadanti mohātte bhūtavidyāviṣayādapohyāḥ//

SL5690/ te tu tatsattvasaṃsargādvijñeyāstu tadañjanāḥ//

SL5696/ hiṃsāvihārā ye keciddevabhāvabhupāśritāḥ†//

SL5698/ grahasaṃjñāni bhūtāni yasmādvettyanayā bhiṣak//
SSu.6.60.28/ vidyayā bhūtavidyātvamata eva nirucyate/
SL5700/ teṣāṃ śāntyarthamanvicchan vaidyastu susamāhitaḥ//
SSu.6.60.29/ japaiḥ saniyamairhomairārabheta cikitsitum/
SL5702/ raktāni gandhamālyāni bījāni madhusarpiṣī//

SL5704/ vastrāṇi gandhamālyāni māṃsāni† rudhirāṇi ca//

SL5706/ hiṃsanti manujān yeṣu prāyaśo divaseṣu tu//
SSu.6.60.32/ dineṣu teṣu deyāni tadbhūtavinivṛttaye/
SL5708/ devagrahe devagṛhe hutvā+agniṃ prāpayed(dāpayed)balim//

SL5710/ asurāya yathākālaṃ vidadhyāccatvarādiṣu//

SL5712/ hṛdye veśmani yakṣasya kulmāṣāsṛksurādibhiḥ//
SSu.6.60.35/ atimuktakakundābjaiḥ puṣpaiśca vitaredbalim/
SL5714/ nadyāṃ pitṛgrahāyeṣṭaṃ kuśāstaraṇabhūṣitam//

SL5716/ catuṣpathe rākṣasasya bhīmeṣu gahaneṣu vā//

SSu.6.60.38/ na śakyā balibhirjetuṃ yogaistān samupācaret/

SL5743/ siṃhavyāghrarkṣamārjāradvīpivājigavāṃ tathā//

<B 61>

ekaṣaṣṭitamo+adhyāyaḥ/
SSu.6.61.3/ smṛtirbhūtārthavijñānamapaśca parivarjane/
SL5759/ apasmāra iti proktastato+ayaṃ vyādhirantakṛt//
SSu.6.61.4/ mithyātiyogendriyārthakarmaṇāmabhisevanāt/
SL5761/ viruddhamalināhāravihārakupitairmalaiḥ//
SSu.6.61.5/ veganigrahaśīlānāmahitāśucibhojinām/
SL5763/ rajastamobhibhūtānāṃ gacchatāṃ ca rajasvalām//
SSu.6.61.6/ tathā kāmabhayodvegakrodhaśokādibhirbhṛśam/
SL5765/ cetasyabhihate puṃsāmapasmāro+abhijāyate//

SL5767/ nidrānāśaśca tasmiṃstu bhaviṣyati bhavantyatha//
SSu.6.61.8/ saṃjñāvaheṣu srotaḥsu doṣavyāpteṣu mānavaḥ/
SL5769/ rajastamaḥparīteṣu mūḍho bhrāntena cetasā//
SSu.6.61.9/ vikṣipan hastapādaṃ ca vijihmabhrūrvilocanaḥ/
SL5771/ dantān khādan vaman phenaṃ vivṛtākṣaḥ patet kṣitau//
SSu.6.61.10/ alpakālānataraṃ cāpi punaḥ saṃjñāṃ labheta saḥ/
SL5773/ so+apasmāra iti proktaḥ sa ca dṛṣṭaścaturvidhaḥ//
SSu.6.61.11/ vātapittakaphairnṝṇāṃ caturthaḥ sannipātataḥ/
SL5775/ vepamāno daśan dantān śvasan phenaṃ vamannapi//
SSu.6.61.12/ yo brūyādvikṛtaṃ sattvaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ māmanudhāvati/
SL5777/ tato me cittanāśaḥ syāt so+apasmāro+anilātmakaḥ//
SSu.6.61.13/ tṛṭtāpasvedamūrcchārto dhunvannaṅgāni vihvalaḥ/
SL5779/ yo brūyādvikṛtaṃ sattvaṃ pītaṃ māmanudhāvati//

SL5781/ śītahṛllāsanidrārtaḥ patan bhūmau vaman kapham//

SL5783/ tato me cittanāśaḥ syāt so+apasmāraḥ kaphātmakaḥ//
SSu.6.61.16/ hṛdi todastṛḍutkledastriṣvapyeteṣu saṃkhyayā/
SL5785/ pralāpaḥ kūjanaṃ kleśaḥ pratyekaṃ tu bhavediha//
SSu.6.61.17/ sarvaliṅgasamavāyaḥ sarvadoṣaprakopaje/
SL5787/ animittāgamādvyādhergamanādakṛte+api ca//
SSu.6.61.18/ āgamāccāpyapasmāraṃ vadantyanye na doṣajam/
SL5789/ kramopayogāddoṣāṇāṃ kṣaṇikatvāttathaiva ca//
SSu.6.61.19/ āgamādvaiśvarūpyācca sa tu nirvarṇyate budhaiḥ/
SL5791/ deve varṣatyapi yathā bhūmau bījāni kānicit//
SSu.6.61.20/ śaradi pratirohanti tathā vyādhisamudbhavaḥ/
SL5793/ sthāyinaḥ kecidalpena kālenābhipravardhitāḥ//
SSu.6.61.21/ darśayanti vikārāṃstu viśvarūpānnisargataḥ/
SL5795/ apasmāro mahāvyādhistasmāddoṣaja eva tu//
SSu.6.61.22/ tasya kāryo vidhiḥ sarvo ya unmādeṣu vakṣyate/
SL5797/ purāṇasarpiṣaḥ pānamabhyaṅgaścaiva pūjitaḥ//
SSu.6.61.23/ upayogo grahoktānāṃ yogānāṃ tu viśeṣataḥ/
SL5799/ tataḥ sidhyanti te sarve yogairanyaiśca sādhayet/
SL5800/ śigrukaṭvaṅgakiṇvāhinimbatvagrasasādhitam//
SSu.6.61.24/ caturguṇe gavāṃ mūtre tailābhyañjane hitam/
SL5802/ godhānakulanāgānāṃ pṛṣatarkṣagavāmapi//
SSu.6.61.25/ pitteṣu siddhaṃ tailaṃ ca pānābhyaṅgeṣu pūjitam/
SL5804/ tīkṣṇairubhayatobhāgaiḥ śiraścāpi viśodhayet//
SSu.6.61.26/ pūjāṃ rudrasya kurvīta tadgaṇānāṃ ca nityaśaḥ/
SL5806/ vātikaṃ bastibhiścāpi paittikaṃ tu virecanaiḥ//
SSu.6.61.27/ kaphajaṃ vamanairdhīmānapasmāramupācaret/
SL5808/ kulatthayavakolāni śaṇabījaṃ palaṅkaṣām//
SSu.6.61.28/ jaṭilāṃ pañcamūlyau dve pathyāṃ cotkvāthya yatnataḥ/

SSu.6.66.10/ traya eva pṛthak doṣā dviśo nava samādhikaiḥ/
SL6183/ trayodaśādhikaikadvisamamadhyolbaṇaistriśaḥ//
SSu.6.66.11/ pañcāśadevaṃ tu saha bhavanti kṣayamāgataiḥ/
SL6185/ †kṣīṇamadhyādhikakṣīṇakṣīṇavṛddhaistathā+aparaiḥ//
SSu.6.66.12/ dvādaśaivaṃ samākhyātāstrayo doṣā dviṣaṣṭidhā/
SL6187/ miśrā dhātumalairdoṣā yāntyasaṃkhyeyatāṃ punaḥ//
SSu.6.66.13/ tasmāt prasaṅgaṃ saṃyamya doṣabhedavikalpanaiḥ/
SL6189/ rogaṃ viditvopacaredrasabhedairyathairitaiḥ//
SSu.6.66.14/ bhiṣak kartā+atha karaṇaṃ rasā doṣāstu kāraṇam/
SL6191/ kāryamārogyamevaikamanārogyamato+anyathā//
SSu.6.66.15/ adhyāyānāṃ tu ṣaṭṣaṣṭyā grathitārthapadakramam/
SL6193/ evametadaśeṣeṇa tantramuttaramṛddhimat//
SSu.6.66.16/ spaṣṭagūḍhārthavijñānamagāḍhamandacetasām/
SL6195/ yathāvidhi yathāpraśnaṃ bhavatāṃ parikīrtitam//
SSu.6.66.17/ sahottaraṃ tvetadadhītya sarvaṃ brāhmaṃ vidhānena yathoditena/
SL6197/ na hīyate+arthānmanaso+abhyupetādetadvaco brahmamatīva satyam//
